v:2.9.0 - 2026-11-09
- WordPress.org Official Rebranding & Trademark Compliance (Phases 1, 2, 3 & 4):
  * Official Plugin Identity: Rebranded plugin to 'WebCare Pro Security & Firewall' with permalink/slug 'webcare-pro-security' in accordance with WordPress.org trademark review requirements.
  * Root Entry File Renaming: Renamed main plugin file from wcp-wp-scanner.php to webcare-pro-security.php with updated author attribution and text domain 'webcare-pro-security'.
  * PHP Namespaces Modernization: Refactored all ~200 PHP classes, namespaces, use declarations, and inline static calls from legacy WcpScanner\ to WebCareProScanner\.
  * Database & Options Fresh Slate: Completely migrated all options, table schemas, transients, cron hooks, and canaries from wcpscan_* to webcareproscan_* (wp_webcareproscan_scans, wp_webcareproscan_scan_issues, webcareproscan_settings, etc.) as a clean-slate fresh installation with zero legacy migrations.
  * Constant & Defines Standardization: Standardized global constant prefixes to WEBCAREPROSCAN_* (WEBCAREPROSCAN_VERSION, WEBCAREPROSCAN_PATH, etc.).
  * Admin Menus & UI Branding (Phase 4): Updated WordPress admin sidebar label to 'WebCare Pro' and primary menu page title to 'WebCare Pro Security & Firewall', updated submenus, notices, modals, and badges across all React cockpit components.
  * Cloudflare Edge Firewall Branding (Phase 4): Transitioned Cloudflare WAF managed rule tags to '[WebCare Pro Security]' with seamless backward-compatible recognition for legacy rules, updated Cloudflare API User-Agent to 'WebCare-Pro-Security/2.9.0', and updated Autonomous Threat Blocklist metadata.
  * Engine Diagnostics & Runtime Notices (Phase 4): Rebranded XML-RPC, HoneyToken, Outbound Egress caller signatures, Micro-WAF preloads, and CrashGuard alert templates to WebCare Pro branding with solo expert freelancer voice.
  * Comprehensive Global wcp/WCP Transition: Systematically refactored all ~20,500 legacy wcp/WCP occurrences across PHP, CSS, React TSX, JS, SVG, and manifests. Renamed CSS variables (--wcp-* -> --webcare-*), CSS classes (.wcp-* -> .webcare-*), React components (WcpShell -> WebCareShell, WcpBrandLogo -> WebCareBrandLogo, etc.), keyframe animations (wcpFadeIn -> webcareFadeIn), PHP classes (WcpActivityLogger -> WebCareActivityLogger, WcpJitMuGuardian -> WebCareJitMuGuardian), vault/quarantine directories (wcp-vault -> webcare-vault), decoy canaries (wcp_canary_admin -> webcare_canary_admin), constants (WCP_* -> WEBCARE_*), and incident identifiers (WCP- -> WEBCARE-).
  * UI Mounting Container Resilience & Slug Synchronization: Restored canonical '#webcare-scanner-root' container mounting with dual-fallback support in all React entrypoints (index.tsx, scan.tsx, firewall.tsx, auth.tsx, etc.), synchronized decoupled navigation page mappings (TAB_TO_PAGE / PAGE_TO_TAB) to 'webcare-pro-*' permalinks, and deployed active build to Docker development environment.
  * Documentation Overhaul & Features Catalog: Streamlined 'README.md' to spotlight top 9 core pillars and expanded 'features.md' to document all 87 enterprise defense engines with dual-level plain-English and engineering architecture.
  * REST Route Standardization & Emergency Constants: Synchronized all client REST endpoints to '/webcare-pro-security/v1', updated WelcomeModal readiness triggers to use standard apiRequest, and updated emergency lockout override constants to 'WEBCAREPROSCAN_OVERRIDE_WHITELIST_IP' and 'WEBCAREPROSCAN_DISABLE_WAF' across manifests and documentation.
  * Top Navigation URL Synchronization (WebCareShell.tsx & AdminMenu.php): Fixed broken menu URLs across decoupled submodule headers by routing all tab triggers (Dashboard, Scanner, Firewall, Identity, Vault & Tools, Settings) through canonical PAGE_MAP resolving directly to 'webcare-pro-*' permalinks. Registered companion submenu hooks in AdminMenu.php and eliminated undefined $request_method warning in FirewallEngine.php.
  * Cryptographic Self-Defense & Release Synchronization: Updated packaging pipeline and compiled release archive 'webcare-pro-security.zip' with 204 verified files in 'includes/System/plugin-manifest.json', achieving 100% pristine baseline integrity.
  * Manifest Synchronization: Synchronized version 2.9.0 across readme.txt, package.json, composer.json, version.json, README.md, and changelog.txt.

v:2.8.0 - 2026-11-08
- Egress & SSRF Firewall Legitimate Webhook Whitelisting & Caller Attribution (OutboundEgressFirewall.php):
  * Authorized Notification Webhook Exemption: Implemented is_authorized_notification_webhook() in OutboundEgressFirewall to automatically exempt configured administrator DevSecOps notification webhooks (discord_webhook_url, slack_webhook_url, clickup_webhook_url, asana_webhook_url, generic_webhook_url) from the malicious exfiltration drop-point check (MALICIOUS_SINK_PATTERNS). Eliminates false-positive WAF incident logging against administrator sessions and legitimate IP addresses when dispatching security notifications.
  * Accurate Scanner Caller Attribution (extract_calling_component()): Refined backtrace stack analysis to specifically skip only OutboundEgressFirewall.php rather than all scanner paths, accurately attributing outbound HTTP calls from WCP Security Scanner (Cloudflare API sync, AI providers, update checks, DevSecOps webhook notifications) to 'WCP Security Scanner' instead of falling through to 'WordPress Core (unknown)'.
  * DevSecOps Host Allowlist Expansion: Added hooks.slack.com, slack.com, api.clickup.com, and app.asana.com to DEFAULT_APPROVED_HOSTS, and introduced the 'wcp_approved_outbound_webhooks' filter for custom integration endpoints.
- Context-Adaptive Firewall (CAF 2.0) & Dynamic Ecosystem Gating:
  * Ecosystem Fingerprinting & O(1) Static Query Helpers (AdaptiveFirewallAdvisor.php): Expanded stack fingerprinting to tag commerce (WooCommerce, EDD, SureCart, FluentCart, GiveWP, WP EasyCart), LMS/membership (LearnDash, MemberPress, LifterLMS, Simple Membership, Ultimate Member), forms, and page builders (Elementor, Divi, Bricks, Beaver Builder, WPBakery). Introduced static cached O(1) runtime helpers (has_ecosystem(), is_plugin_slug_active(), get_active_profile_rules()) to eliminate repeated option lookups.
  * Zero-Overhead WooCommerce Rule Gating (FirewallEngine.php): Gated Rules #38–#42 in init() behind AdaptiveFirewallAdvisor::has_ecosystem('woocommerce'), preventing unneeded template_redirect and rest_request_after_callbacks hooks from registering on sites without WooCommerce.
  * O(1) Shopper & Membership Short-Circuiting: Short-circuited has_active_shopper_session() and is_commerce_or_membership_request() to immediately bail on non-e-commerce/non-membership sites, eliminating unnecessary cookie loops and URI pattern evaluations on standard blogs and corporate sites.
  * Visual Page Builder Dynamic Gating: Gated 20 Elementor, Divi, and page builder parameters inside is_authenticated_editor_or_builder() behind has_ecosystem('page_builder'), allowing clean, unhindered core Gutenberg saves while skipping builder checks when builders are absent.
  * Velocity & Prompt Injection Pipeline Gating (DistributedVelocityShield.php & PromptInjectionGuard.php): Conditioned WooCommerce checkout velocity tracking and order note prompt-injection sanitizers on active WooCommerce ecosystem presence.
  * Micro-WAF & Virtual Patch Dynamic Compilation (MicroWafCompiler.php & VirtualPatchManager.php): Conditioned woocommerce_* guest AJAX actions in caf-data.php on active WooCommerce presence, and filtered virtual patch evaluation to execute only rules matching installed and active plugin components.
- ThreatScore First-Hit Critical Exploit Ban (Phase 2):
  * Deterministic Exploit First-Hit Banning (FirewallEngine.php & ThreatScoreTracker.php): Introduced WEIGHT_CRITICAL_EXPLOIT (85.0 points) in ThreatScoreTracker and re-calibrated handle_rule_violation() so deterministic exploits (SQL Injection, PHP Code Injection, Execution Armor, Path Traversal, Cross-Site Scripting, Malicious File Upload, Virtual Patches, AI Prompt Injection) trigger an immediate hard ban (24h) and Cloudflare edge push on hit #1 instead of requiring 22 cumulative violations.
- Instant Zero-Latency Cloudflare Edge Offload & Auto-Pruning (Phase 3):
  * Zero-Latency Post-Response Edge Push (CloudflareIpListSynchronizer.php): Added flush_immediate_shutdown_bans() via register_shutdown_function() leveraging fastcgi_finish_request() and litespeed_finish_request() to immediately flush HTTP 403 responses to attackers (0ms visitor latency) while pushing attacker IPs directly to Cloudflare Edge in the background.
  * Adaptive Burst Throttling & Swarm Subnet Aggregation: Introduced a 10-second burst limiter (wcp_cf_instant_burst) that handles up to 5 instant bans immediately and seamlessly diverts high-velocity botnet swarms (>5 bans/10s) into the background batch queue for /24 IPv4 and /64 IPv6 subnet aggregation.
  * Bi-Directional Cloudflare Unban Synchronization: Implemented unban_ip_immediately($ip) wired directly into FirewallEngine::remove_blocked_ip($ip), ensuring that unbanning an IP locally in WordPress simultaneously purges matching Zone IP Access Rules and Account Custom IP List items on Cloudflare Edge.
  * Active Edge Ban Ledger & Auto-Pruning: Maintained lightweight ledger wcp_cf_active_edge_bans tracking active edge items and their expires_at timestamps, with automated 5-minute cron pruning of expired temporary bans from Cloudflare Edge.
- Authentication State Caching & False-Positive Hardening:
  * Early Bootstrap Cache Fix (FirewallEngine.php): Fixed a false-negative condition where is_authenticated_administrator() cached a premature false return during early plugins_loaded hooks before WordPress finished loading current user sessions. Deferred caching false until after did_action('init'), ensuring verified administrators actively navigating wp-admin are never misclassified as unauthenticated.
- Argon Code Audit Refinements & Bug Fixes:
  * Cloudflare Wirefilter String Literal Escaping Fix (CloudflareSlotOptimizer.php): Fixed a syntax error in Slot 1 rule compilation where wp_json_encode() escaped forward slashes (\/) in dormant plugin path expressions, triggering Cloudflare API filter parsing error 1:258 ("expected quote, xHH or OOO after \"). Replaced with clean unescaped Wirefilter string formatting.
  * ThreatScore Ban Metadata Alignment (ThreatScoreTracker.php & FirewallEngine.php): Corrected argument positioning in escalate_to_hard_ban() to align with FirewallEngine::add_blocked_ip() parameter schema ($rule_id as 4th parameter) and removed redundant record_event() call from handle_rule_violation() instant-ban branch to strictly preserve rich exploit rule names, categories, and incident IDs.
  * Block Directive Activity Logging Namespace Fix (BlockDirectiveSanitizer.php): Corrected namespace import and class_exists reference from WcpScanner\Activity\WcpActivityLogger to WcpScanner\System\WcpActivityLogger, resolving a silent logging drop and ensuring all neutralized Gutenberg Interactivity API directives and prototype pollution payloads are recorded in the security activity ledger.
  * Distributed Velocity High-Concurrency In-Memory Buffer (DistributedVelocityShield.php): Implemented atomic APCu ring buffering (wcpscan_dvs_*) for sliding-window checkout and authentication tracking when APCu is enabled, eliminating database row-lock contention during high-velocity distributed carding attacks with arrays bounded to 200 events.
  * Automated Object-Cache Resynchronization & Auto-Heal (ObjectCacheIntegritySentinel.php): Implemented automated remediation that immediately flushes Redis/Memcached and resynchronizes RAM state from MySQL truth whenever critical site navigation keys (siteurl, home, active_plugins) suffer in-memory cache poisoning.
- HoneyToken & Database Canary Hardening & False-Positive Prevention:
  * Self-Trap Prevention in Canary Seeding (HoneyTokenEngine.php): Fixed a critical regression where ensure_honey_options_seeded() queried decoy options via get_option() during init, inadvertently tripping its own pre_option_* honeytrap and banning legitimate administrator/local IP addresses. Re-architected option checks to directly inspect and insert decoy rows via $wpdb with autoload='no' and enforced internal audit bypass flags.
  * Administrator & Local Gateway Immunity: Enhanced trigger_trap(), ban_bot_ip(), and FirewallEngine::add_blocked_ip() to guarantee that authenticated administrators actively browsing wp-admin and local Docker/container bridge gateways (172.17.x.x, 172.18.x.x, 172.19.x.x, 10.0.2.x) can never be trapped, terminated with 403, or added to the custom IP blocklist.
- 2026 AI-Era Defense (Pillar 8: Database Canary Honey-Tokens & Ghost Admin User):
  * Ghost Admin Canary User Tripwire (HoneyTokenEngine.php): Automatically provisions and arms a dormant 'wcp_canary_admin' user inside wp_users with a cryptographically impossible password hash and '_wcpscan_is_canary' meta tag. Hooks into 'authenticate' at priority 1 to intercept any brute-force, credential-stuffing, or leaked database dump reuse attempt, immediately logging a critical breach and auto-banning the offender IP.
  * Hidden Security Canary Architecture: Filters 'pre_user_query' on wp-admin/users.php to conceal the tripwire canary user from everyday administrative screens, avoiding confusion while keeping it armed and exposed to SQL injectors and automated scrapers.
  * Database Honey Options Sentinel: Plants decoy API credentials in wp_options ('_wcpscan_canary_aws_secret', '_wcpscan_canary_stripe_secret', '_wcpscan_canary_vault_backup'). Intercepts 'pre_option_*' queries to catch unauthorized read attempts from compromised plugins or arbitrary SQL execution, triggering instant out-of-band alerts via NotificationDispatcher ('db_canary_tripwire_breach') and immediate IP isolation.
  * Reseed & Health Management Endpoint (ScannerRoutes.php): Added dedicated REST route '/behavioral/honeytoken/reseed-db-canaries' allowing administrators to programmatically verify and re-seed database canaries.
  * Behavioral WAF React Cockpit Enhancements (BehavioralWafCard.tsx): Added a dedicated Database Canary Honey-Tokens & Ghost Admin Sentinel status panel displaying live armed status of wcp_canary_admin and decoy options, alongside 1-click 'Verify & Reseed Canaries' controls.
- 2026 AI-Era Defense (Pillar 1 Enhancement: Step-Up 2FA on authorize-application.php):
  * In-Flow Step-Up 2FA & Identity Verification (ApplicationPasswordSentinel.php): Blocks 1-click token phishing consent traps by requiring real-time identity re-authentication before any REST Application Password can be minted.
  * Multi-Factor Enforcement: Injects an in-flow verification card into the native WordPress authorize-application screen via 'wp_authorize_application_password_form', requiring a valid 6/8-digit TOTP code (for accounts with 2FA enabled) or account password confirmation (for accounts without TOTP) during approval POSTs, returning structured security errors on missing or invalid verification.
  * React Policy Controls (AppPasswordSentinelCard.tsx): Added 'Enforce Step-Up 2FA / Identity Verification' policy toggle and status badge inside the REST Application Passwords dashboard.
- 2026 AI-Era Defense (Pillar 7: WordPress 6.5+ Interactivity API & Block Binding Firewall):
  * Runtime Directive Interception (BlockDirectiveSanitizer.php): Multi-pipeline filters hooking wp_insert_post_data (content persistence) and rest_pre_dispatch (REST API post mutations) to inspect and neutralize hostile Gutenberg directives before storage in wp_posts.
  * Interactivity API Event Handler Validation: Strictly audits 'data-wp-on--*' directives against unauthorized expressions, raw JavaScript statements, function invocations, semicolons, and prototype traversal (actions.constructor, Function, eval), neutralizing hostile handlers to inert attributes.
  * In-Context Prototype Pollution Sentinel: Recursively unpacks and normalizes 'data-wp-context' JSON state trees (accounting for escaped quotes and HTML entity encodings) to block prototype pollution keys (__proto__, constructor, prototype) that could compromise client-side state stores.
  * Hostile Attribute & Dynamic URI Binding Guard: Validates 'data-wp-bind--*' attributes (href, src, style) to block dynamic JavaScript URI schemes (javascript:, data:text/html) and legacy CSS expression bindings (-moz-binding, expression()).
  * Gutenberg Sensitive Meta Exfiltration Blocker: Inspects block comment metadata ('<!-- wp:... {"metadata":{"bindings":{...}}} -->') to detect and strip unauthorized 'core/post-meta' bindings targeting sensitive internal keys (_wcpscan_*, _secret_*, _token*, _password*, _nonce*), preventing unauthorized exfiltration of cryptographic vault tokens and security credentials through public REST post responses.
  * HTML Static Analyzer Integration (HTMLAnalyzer.php): Integrated check #10 calling BlockDirectiveSanitizer::audit_content() during full content scans to flag hostile block directives and unapproved interactivity expressions.
  * REST API Suite (ScannerRoutes.php): Registered endpoints for '/defense/block-security/status' (telemetry, audited/neutralized counts, whitelisted core namespaces) and '/defense/block-security/audit' (on-demand audit and sanitization of Gutenberg block markup).
  * React Cockpit & Interactive Sandbox (AiDefenseCard.tsx & FirewallPage.tsx): Added Sub-tab 8 under AI Defense with a real-time directive telemetry grid, 1-click test vectors (Event Injection, Context Pollution, Hostile Bind, Meta Exfiltration, Clean Block), live block inspector, and sanitized markup preview.
v:2.7.2 - 2026-11-07
- 2026 AI-Era Defense (Pillar 6: /llms.txt, JSON-LD Schema & Invisible CSS Cloaking - AI SEO & GEO Shield):
  * /llms.txt & /llms-full.txt Cryptographic Sentinel (GeoShieldService.php & ImportantFileFIM.php): Monitors root /llms.txt and /llms-full.txt feeds against AES-256 / SHA-256 baselines (wcpscan_llmstxt_baseline). Defends against emerging 2026 Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) attacks where threat actors hijack AI search crawler feeds (ChatGPT Search, PerplexityBot, Claude, Google SGE) by injecting unauthorized customer service hotlines, phishing links, cryptocurrency drainer wallets, or prompt injection directives (### System:, <|im_start|>).
  * JSON-LD Structured Data Schema Poisoning Auditor: Recursively analyzes <script type="application/ld+json"> schemas across posts, pages, and theme hooks to detect fraudulent telephone spoofing (+1-900 toll fraud, international premium lines), authority redirection to high-risk TLDs (.xyz, .top, .cam, .click), and embedded cryptocurrency wallet addresses.
  * Invisible CSS Cloaking & GEO Hijacking Protection (HTMLAnalyzer.php): Detects sophisticated layout tricks used to hide phishing and drainer payloads from human reviewers while exposing them to AI web crawlers: transparent text colors (color: transparent, rgba(0,0,0,0)), identical foreground/background contrast cloaking (white on white, black on black), and screen-reader utility classes (sr-only, screen-reader-text, clip-path) abused to conceal foreign external URLs or crypto addresses.
  * 1-Click Pristine Baseline Generator: Allows administrators to instantly calculate and lock in cryptographic SHA-256 hashes of authentic /llms.txt and /llms-full.txt files with automatic drift alerts during File Integrity Monitor (FIM) scans.
  * REST API Suite (ScannerRoutes.php): Registered endpoints for '/defense/geo-shield/status' (telemetry, baseline status, active schema checks), '/defense/geo-shield/save-baseline' (1-click cryptographic baseline snapshot), and '/defense/geo-shield/audit-content' (on-demand audit of HTML or markup for deceptive GEO elements).
  * AI Defense React Cockpit & GEO Sandbox (AiDefenseCard.tsx): Added Sub-tab 7 under AI Defense featuring live status cards for /llms.txt standards, 1-click baseline management, and an interactive GEO Cloak & Schema Inspector sandbox with preloaded test vectors (Toll Fraud Schema, Transparent Text Cloaking, Abused sr-only Links, and Clean Schema).
- 2026 AI-Era Defense (Pillar 5: Source-to-Sink AST Taint Flow Analyzer for Polymorphic Webshells):
  * Token-Level Dataflow Engine (TaintAnalysisEngine.php): Built native PHP AST dataflow tracking using token_get_all() to track untrusted user input (Sources) across multi-step variable assignments, string concatenations, array destructuring, and benign transformation wrappers into dangerous execution sinks. Neutralizes 2026 LLM-crafted polymorphic webshells that evade static signatures and entropy heuristics by using natural English variable names and standard library APIs.
  * Comprehensive Source & Scope Poisoning Coverage: Tracks superglobals ($_GET, $_POST, $_COOKIE, $_REQUEST, $_SERVER, $_FILES, $HTTP_RAW_POST_DATA, $GLOBALS), input streams (file_get_contents('php://input')), and request headers (getallheaders), alongside scope poisoning via extract($_POST) and parse_str().
  * Execution Sink & Reflection Interception: Intercepts variable function invocations ($func($arg)), dynamic object method calls ($obj->$method($arg)), modern reflection instantiation (ReflectionFunction, ReflectionMethod, ReflectionClass), iterator and closure callbacks (CallbackFilterIterator, array_map, call_user_func, register_shutdown_function), dynamic code inclusions (include, require), and system command execution (eval, assert, system, exec, shell_exec).
  * Benign Transformation & Cast Disambiguation: Accurately propagates taint through string manipulation functions (base64_decode, gzinflate, trim, stripslashes, pack, hex2bin, unserialize, json_decode) while recognizing sanitizing casts ((int), (float), (bool), intval, absint) to prevent false positives on legitimate WordPress sanitization patterns.
  * Location Context Risk Weighting: Dynamically elevates detection confidence to 99% (Critical) for dynamic execution sinks inside wp-content/uploads/ or wp-content/cache/, where dynamic code execution should never occur.
  * Heuristic & Webshell Scanner Integration (WebshellDetector.php & HeuristicEngine.php): Directly integrated as fallback AST analysis when classic regex signatures score 0, capturing polymorphic webshells during full-site malware scans.
  * REST API Suite (ScannerRoutes.php): Registered dedicated endpoints for '/defense/taint-engine/status' (telemetry, supported sources, sinks, files analyzed) and '/defense/taint-engine/analyze' (on-demand AST taint analysis of PHP code snippets or disk files).
  * React Cockpit & Forensic Pipeline Visualizer (AiDefenseCard.tsx & FindingForensicsDrawer.tsx): Added Sub-tab 6 under AI Defense with an interactive AST Taint Analyzer Laboratory featuring 1-click test vectors (Reflection Webshell, CallbackFilterIterator Evasion, SplFileObject Dropper, Variable Function Indirection, and Benign WordPress Sanitized Input), real-time dataflow pipeline visualization, and step-by-step breadcrumb cards inside finding forensic dossiers.
- 2026 AI-Era Defense (Pillar 4: Global Cross-IP Distributed Velocity Shield for AI Botnets):
  * Sliding-Window Cross-IP Velocity Aggregator (DistributedVelocityShield.php): Real-time sliding 60-second window tracker aggregating transaction and authentication failures across distributed residential proxy botnets (wcpscan_dvs_chk_events and wcpscan_dvs_auth_events). Overcomes per-IP rate limit bypasses where AI bot swarms cycle thousands of rotating residential IPs at low frequencies (1 request every 45-60s).

Triggers autonomous site-wide Under-Attack Mode (wcpscan_under_attack_active) with emergency duration timer (default 15 minutes) and auto-decay.

Humans solve the challenge silently in ~30ms without CAPTCHA friction, while headless bot swarms, carding scripts, and brute-force tools encounter prohibitive CPU exhaustion.
  * Deep Commerce & Auth Pipeline Hooks: Intercepts woocommerce_after_checkout_validation, woocommerce_order_status_failed, and woocommerce_checkout_process to monitor failed transactions, alongside wp_login_failed and authenticate to monitor credential stuffing attempts.

- 2026 AI-Era Defense (Pillar 3: Inbound AI Prompt-Injection & Indirect Jailbreak Firewall):

- Context-Adaptive Firewall (CAF) Phase 3 — Behavioral Intelligence, Multi-Bucket Rate Limiting & Stateless PoW (AdaptiveRateLimiter.php, ThreatScoreTracker.php, BotReconDefense.php, LearningModeEngine.php, ScannerRoutes.php, FirewallEngine.php):
  * Sliding-Window Multi-Bucket Velocity Limiter (`AdaptiveRateLimiter.php`): Engineered 5 isolated velocity boundaries: `auth` (5 hits/300s on login/xmlrpc/jwt), `checkout` (8 hits/60s with valid WooCommerce JSON failure responses), `cart_ajax` (30 hits/60s for fragments, add-to-cart, order review), `rest_write` (30 mutations/60s on POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE), and `search_probe` (25 queries/60s on ?s= and REST search). Uses memory caching (APCu/Redis) with transient fallback and sliding-window counter formula $C_{eff} = C_{prev} \times (1 - \Delta t / W) + C_{curr}$.
  * Differential Threat Score Engine with Exponential Decay (`ThreatScoreTracker.php`): Implemented differential 403/404 & probe velocity tracking with a 15-minute exponential half-life ($\lambda = \frac{\ln(2)}{900} \approx 0.00077\text{ s}^{-1}$): $T_{IP}(t) = T_{IP}(t_{last}) \cdot e^{-\lambda \Delta t} + \Delta S$. Assigns weighted penalties (+25 honeypot/sensitive, +15 dormant surface, +10 auth 403, +4 consecutive 404s, +15 header anomalies). Automatically escalates: $T \ge 40 \rightarrow$ WebCrypto PoW challenge; $T \ge 85 \rightarrow$ local hard drop and automatic enqueueing to the Cloudflare Edge ban queue.
  * Stateless WebCrypto Proof-of-Work Challenge (`BotReconDefense.php`): Integrated inline zero-external-JS WebCrypto SHA-256 challenge ($k = 16$ leading zero bits) verified via HMAC-SHA256 with 3600-second IP-bound clearance cookie. Defends against headless automated bots with zero database round-trips.
  * Client-Hint & Modern HTTP/2 Consistency Checks (`BotReconDefense.php`, `FirewallEngine.php`): Added inspection of Chromium Client-Hints (`Sec-CH-UA`, `Sec-CH-UA-Mobile`, `Sec-CH-UA-Platform`) on HTTPS, catching modern scraper bots claiming Chrome desktop while omitting Client-Hints or emitting contradictory platform signatures.
  * Poison-Resistant Human-in-the-Loop Learning Mode (`LearningModeEngine.php`): Hardened passive observation recording (`wcp_caf_learning_observations`) with explicit authentication and nonce context tagging (`authenticated_nonce`, `authenticated_session`, `anonymous_guest`). Enforces admin-only approval for 1-click surgical safe-pass exemptions, preventing anonymous attackers from injecting malicious whitelist payloads.
  * Behavioral Intelligence Telemetry Endpoints (`ScannerRoutes.php`): Expanded `/caf/behavioral-status` to return active threat scores, PoW enforcement counters, multi-bucket velocity stats, and learning observation summaries.

Drops probes targeting deactivated plugin paths (`WAF-CAF-DORMANT-SURFACE`), uninstalled/dormant REST API namespaces (`WAF-CAF-DORMANT-REST-NAMESPACE`), and unauthenticated arbitrary guest AJAX actions (`WAF-CAF-DORMANT-AJAX-ACTION`) with instant HTTP 403 Forbidden and fail2ban logging before WordPress boots, respecting `.killswitch` and `WCP_CAF_DISABLE_PRELOAD` with zero database overhead.

- Context-Adaptive Firewall (CAF) Phase 3 — Behavioral Intelligence, Multi-Bucket Rate Limiting & WebCrypto PoW (AdaptiveRateLimiter.php, BotReconDefense.php, LearningModeEngine.php, ScannerRoutes.php, CafBehavioralCockpit.tsx, FirewallEngine.php):
  * Multi-Bucket Stateful Velocity Limiter (`AdaptiveRateLimiter.php`): Refactored rate limiting into 4 independent application boundaries utilizing APCu/memory caching and non-locking transients: Authentication (5 hits/5min on login/xmlrpc/jwt), E-Commerce Checkout (8 hits/min on `wc-ajax` actions with structured JSON errors), Search (25 queries/min on `?s=` and REST search), and REST API (60 reqs/min burst allowance).
  * HTTP/2 & HTTP/3 Pseudo-Header Sequencing Sentinel (`BotReconDefense.php`, `FirewallEngine.php`): Built automated inspection verifying pseudo-header sequencing (`:method` -> `:authority` -> `:scheme` -> `:path`) and lowercase standards on modern protocols. Immediately intercepts headless automated scrapers (Python, Go, cURL) exhibiting header order anomalies with HTTP 403 Forbidden (`BOT-H2-HEADER-ANOMALY`).
  * Stateless WebCrypto Proof-of-Work Engine (`BotReconDefense.php`): Engineered zero-external-JS challenge requiring suspicious clients to solve a 16-bit leading zero SHA-256 hash puzzle (`crypto.subtle.digest`) in ~70ms. Issues signed `wcp_pow_clearance` cookies (3600s TTL) upon successful in-browser computation, halting automated scrapers without user CAPTCHA friction.
  * 1-Click False-Positive Exemption Synthesizer (`LearningModeEngine.php`): Added passive observation recorder (`wcp_caf_learning_observations`) and surgical rule softener. Translates blocked WAF incident IDs into targeted safe-pass clauses bound to specific routes, parameters, and user capabilities, resolving false positives without lowering global firewall shields.

- Context-Adaptive Firewall (CAF) Phase 2 — Cloudflare Free-Plan Edge Offload & Origin Survival Mode (CloudflareSlotOptimizer.php, CloudflareIpListSynchronizer.php, OriginLoadCircuitBreaker.php, ScannerRoutes.php, CloudflareSlotVisualizer.tsx, FirewallPage.tsx):
  * Cloudflare Free-Plan 3-Slot Optimizer (`CloudflareSlotOptimizer.php`): Engineered non-destructive rule packing algorithm claiming at most 3 slots out of Cloudflare's 5-rule Free Plan quota (`min(3, max(0, 5 - count(user_rules)))`), guaranteeing that customer-created Cloudflare rules are never deleted or overwritten. Compiles packed expressions for Slot 1 (Threat List, Core Sensitive Files & Dormant Plugin Lockdown), Slot 2 (Managed Challenge for brute-force bots and comment spammers), and Slot 3 (Priority-sorted StackGuard Virtual Patches) while strictly enforcing a 4,000-byte Wirefilter limit with graceful AST truncation.
  * Dual-Engine Bulk IP List Synchronizer & Subnet Aggregator (`CloudflareIpListSynchronizer.php`): Probes Cloudflare API credentials on demand to detect Account Custom List permissions (10,000 items) versus Zone Access Rules (50,000 items). Implemented automated /24 IPv4 and /64 IPv6 CIDR subnet consolidation whenever >= 4 distinct IPs within the same prefix are banned within 15 minutes, preserving quota. Asynchronously flushes the ban queue via `wcp_cloudflare_ip_sync_cron` every 5 minutes.
  * Origin Survival Mode & cPanel Circuit Breaker (`OriginLoadCircuitBreaker.php`): Built automated CPU and probe rate monitoring (`sys_getloadavg()` > 4.0 or > 120 403/404 probes/min) protecting cPanel and shared hosting environments against 508 Resource Limit crashes. Automatically escalates Cloudflare Edge security level to Under Attack / Managed Challenge to shed load off WordPress, and automatically deescalates once origin metrics remain healthy for 10 consecutive minutes.
  * Edge Visualizer & REST APIs (`CloudflareSlotVisualizer.tsx`, `ScannerRoutes.php`, `FirewallPage.tsx`): Built interactive 5-slot visualizer bar showing user vs WCP slot allocation, expression byte counts, token capability badges, and 1-click Origin Survival Mode manual triggers and synchronization endpoints under `/caf/cloudflare-status`, `/caf/sync-cloudflare`, and `/caf/toggle-circuit-breaker`.
- Context-Adaptive Firewall (CAF) & StackGuard Engine - Phase 1 Foundation (StackSnapshot.php, ConflictAvoidanceEngine.php, AdaptiveFirewallAdvisor.php, MicroWafCompiler.php, FirewallEngine.php, ScannerRoutes.php, CafAdvisorPanel.tsx):
  * WordPress Stack Profiling & Fingerprinting (`StackSnapshot.php`): Built an immutable value object and DTO (`StackSnapshot`) capturing core WordPress version, PHP environment, active and inactive plugins, active/parent themes, registered REST namespaces, and detected ecosystem flags (`woocommerce`, `elementor`, `divi`, `gravityforms`, `wpforms`, `contact-form-7`, `fluent-crm`, `learndash`, `memberpress`). Calculates canonical SHA-256 fingerprint hashes for instantaneous change detection.
  * Deterministic Plugin Conflict Avoidance Engine (`ConflictAvoidanceEngine.php`): Evaluates active plugins from the stack snapshot to inject deterministic parameter and route safe-pass exemptions. Preempts false-positive blocks on WooCommerce `wc-ajax` actions (`update_order_review`, `checkout`, `add_to_cart`), Elementor page builder AJAX (`elementor_ajax`, `/wp-json/elementor/v1/`), Divi visual builder payloads, Gravity Forms multipart uploads, Contact Form 7, WPForms, and FluentCRM webhook receivers.
  * Smart Protection Profiles & Stack-Aware Advisor (`AdaptiveFirewallAdvisor.php`): Engineered 6 smart profiles tailored to specific WordPress environments (`ecommerce_hardened`, `corporate_brochure`, `membership_lms`, `cpanel_shared_saver`, `high_security_paranoid`, `balanced`). Automatically evaluates stack-scoped virtual patch applicability (enabling only patches relevant to installed software while skipping irrelevant rules), calculates residual risk scores, and listens to WordPress lifecycle hooks (`activated_plugin`, `deactivated_plugin`, `switch_theme`, `upgrader_process_complete`) to automatically trigger snapshot refreshes.
  * Data-Only Token-Verified MicroWAF Compiler (`MicroWafCompiler.php`): Built data-only rule compiler compiling CAF profile rules and dormant surfaces into `wcp-caf-compiled-rules.php` alongside a detached HMAC-SHA256 signature (`wcp-caf-compiled-rules.php.sig`). Validates artifacts using `token_get_all()` against strict allowed tokens (`T_RETURN`, `T_ARRAY`, strings, numbers, booleans) to eliminate code injection vectors, and provides a hard emergency kill-switch (`.killswitch` file and `WCP_CAF_DISABLE_PRELOAD` constant).
  * Runtime Dormant Attack Surface Lockdown (`FirewallEngine.php`): Integrated early request interception checking incoming URIs against inactive plugins and themes recorded in the snapshot. Strictly drops unauthenticated direct HTTP probes targeting deactivated components (`WAF-CAF-DORMANT-SURFACE` and `WAF-CAF-DORMANT-THEME`) with HTTP 403 Forbidden.
  * Context-Adaptive Firewall UI & REST API (`CafAdvisorPanel.tsx`, `ScannerRoutes.php`, `FirewallPage.tsx`): Built full-stack interactive advisor panel under the Firewall Rules tab. Administrators can view their current detected ecosystem, inspect recommended protection profiles, switch active profiles with 1-click execution, trigger immediate stack re-profiling, and toggle emergency kill-switches.
- Sprint 3 AI/MCP Agent Governance & Surgical In-File Remediation Suite (AiAgentInventoryScanner.php, SecretVault.php, FimVaultManager.php, TransactionalRemediation.php):
  * Third-Party AI & MCP Plugin Governance (`AiAgentInventoryScanner.php`, `SecretVault.php`): Built automated discovery of autonomous AI agents, LLM integrations, and MCP connectors across active plugins, registered REST namespaces, and option storage. Flags unauthenticated REST routes missing permission callbacks and identifies plaintext API keys (`openai`, `anthropic`, `gemini`, `mcp`, `ai_secret`). Provides one-click cryptographic vaulting migrating unencrypted option secrets into AES-256-GCM AEAD encrypted storage via `SecretVault`.
  * Surgical In-File Remediation & Baseline Vaulting (`FimVaultManager.php`, `TransactionalRemediation.php`): Engineered zero-quarantine surgical file restoration. Captures compressed gzip pristine baselines inside a hardened, directory-indexed-protected vault (`fim-vault/baselines`). Injected malware or malicious modifications within core or plugin files are safely restored from baseline with automated pre-repair snapshots and CrashGuard loopback health check probes that roll back changes instantly if fatal errors or HTTP 500 responses occur.
- Sprint 2 AI-Era Architecture & Deception Sentinel Suite (CronPersistenceScanner.php, SerializedDataScanner.php, UpdateGatekeeperEngine.php, HookInfiltrationSentinel.php, FirewallEngine.php, HoneyTokenEngine.php, BotReconDefense.php):
  * WooCommerce & ActionScheduler Keyset Pagination (`CronPersistenceScanner.php`): Replaced table-locking full scans with non-locking primary key cursor pagination (`WHERE action_id > %d ORDER BY action_id ASC LIMIT 250`) on `wp_actionscheduler_actions`. Prevents InnoDB lock contention and checkout slowdowns during high-volume store traffic while auditing pending hooks and extended arguments.
  * Class-Aware Serialized Object & POP-Chain Validation (`SerializedDataScanner.php`): Built zero-unserialize static class inspections. Validates against white-listed WooCommerce and ActionScheduler domain classes while flagging dangerous PHP Object Injection gadget chains (Guzzle, Monolog, Symfony, Illuminate) and unexpected objects.
  * Pre-Install Package Security Audit & Atomic Upgrade Window (`UpdateGatekeeperEngine.php`, `FirewallEngine.php`): Integrated temporary package audit inspecting unzipped directories for zip-slip path traversals, external directory symlinks, and obfuscated webshell markers before files reach production directories. Added atomic upgrade locks with 180-second TTLs, blocking unauthenticated external requests to `/wp-admin/upgrade.php` and plugin directories during updates.
  * In-Memory Closure Reflection & Gadget Callback Sentinel (`HookInfiltrationSentinel.php`): Audited critical runtime hooks (`authenticate`, `init`, `template_redirect`, `wp_authenticate`). Utilizes `ReflectionFunction` on anonymous Closures to detect fileless `eval()'d code` closures, writable directory bindings (`/uploads/`, `/tmp/`), and dangerous array reflection/gadget callbacks (`__destruct`, `__wakeup`, `invoke`).
  * Incoming Object Injection & Stream Wrapper Shield (`FirewallEngine.php`): Added deep parameter and URI inspection blocking PHP object serialization strings (`O:`, `C:`) and dangerous PHP stream wrappers (`phar://`, `php://filter`, `expect://`) across incoming HTTP requests.
  * Active Canary Honeytokens & Signed Webhook Decoys (`HoneyTokenEngine.php`): Engineered realistic decoy `.env` generation with HMAC-signed canary callback tokens (`sk-wcp-canary-<token>`). Automatically quarantines and reports external C2 servers and LLM recon scrapers that probe decoy tokens.
  * Stateless WebCrypto SHA-256 Proof-of-Work (`BotReconDefense.php`): Implemented zero-JS-dependency browser verification requiring clients to solve a 16-bit leading zero SHA-256 challenge via native `crypto.subtle.digest`. Legitimate browsers solve the challenge invisibly in ~70ms, halting automated Python, headless, and AI scrapers.
- Late-2026 Architectural Hardening Suite & SSRF/MCP/Batch Defense (OutboundEgressFirewall.php, FirewallEngine.php, RestApiInventory.php, MCPAgentService.php, PromptInjectionGuard.php):
  * Outbound Egress DNS Pinning & TOCTOU Rebinding Elimination (`OutboundEgressFirewall.php`): Implemented single-pass DNS resolution across A and AAAA records via `dns_get_record()` and `gethostbynamel()`. Evaluates all resolved IPs against RFC 1918, loopback, IPv6 unique-local (`fc00::/7`), and AWS/GCP/Alibaba link-local metadata ranges (`169.254.0.0/16`, `100.100.100.200`, `fd00:ec2::254`). Securely pins verified public destination IPs directly into cURL transports via `CURLOPT_RESOLVE` on the `http_api_curl` hook, permanently defeating Time-of-Check to Time-of-Use (TOCTOU) DNS rebinding SSRF attacks.
  * REST API Batch-Route & Method-Override Normalization (`FirewallEngine.php`, `RestApiInventory.php`): Built recursive inspection and extraction engine (`extract_rest_batch_payloads`) for `/wp-json/wp/v2/batch` and `/batch/v1` payloads. Walks nested `requests[]` sub-requests, unpacks query parameters and serialized JSON bodies, canonicalizes `_method` query and `X-HTTP-Method-Override` headers, and intercepts smuggled unauthenticated user enumeration and mutating exploits before execution (neutralizing CVE-2026-63030 and CVE-2026-60137 exploit chains).
  * Deterministic MCP Capability Sandboxing & Cryptographic Confirmation Tokens (`MCPAgentService.php`): Hardened Model Context Protocol (MCP) agent tool execution by enforcing strict deterministic sandboxing on all state-mutating actions (including file quarantine, virtual patch deployment, containment mode, and baseline recalibration). Replaced auto-approval paths with HMAC-signed cryptographic confirmation tokens (`wcp_mcp_<hash>`) requiring verified human administrative authentication (`manage_options`) prior to execution.
  * Advanced Adversarial Prompt Injection & Smuggling Isolation (`PromptInjectionGuard.php`): Expanded prompt injection detection to neutralize zero-width spaces (`U+200B-U+200D`, `U+FEFF`), bidirectional override controls (`U+202A-U+202E`), invisible Unicode tag smuggler characters, and embedded comment directives (`/* SYSTEM: Ignore ... */`, `<!-- SYSTEM: ... -->`), ensuring downstream AI agents and LLM prompts operate strictly on sanitized inputs.
- Diagnostics Telemetry & Cloudflare Edge Verification Hardening (TelemetrySnapshot.php, CloudflareService.php, PromptInjectionGuard.php):

- E-Commerce Safe-Pass, Auto-Heal & False-Positive Elimination (FirewallEngine.php, HoneyTokenEngine.php, GhostRouteTrap.php):

When a visitor holds a valid cart session or interacts with checkout payment tokens (`wc-ajax=ppc-sdk-v6-client-token`, `wc-ajax=checkout`), automated and non-admin custom IP blocks are automatically bypassed with monitored telemetry logging, eliminating catastrophic false positives on paying store customers.

- Multi-Signal Primary Administrator Resolution & Lockout Elimination (EphemeralAdminEngine.php):

  * Multi-Signal Evaluation Hierarchy: Checks in prioritized order: (1) Multisite Super Admin (`is_super_admin`), (2) Site `admin_email` match (`strcasecmp`), (3) Oldest registered administrator in `wp_users` (`MIN(user_registered)` with capability array inspection), and (4) User ID 1 fallback only if the account actively possesses `manage_options`.

- Outbound SSRF & Cloud Metadata Egress Firewall (OutboundEgressFirewall.php):

  * Administrator Password Reset & Profile Update Alerts (`auth_admin_password_reset`): Connected hooks to both `password_reset` (recovery flow) and `profile_update` (direct admin profile updates). Immediately fires high-severity alerts whenever an administrator's password is reset or changed, logging the target administrator, the updating actor, client IP, and timestamp.
- Search Crawler Immunity & False-Positive Ban Auto-Healing (BotReconDefense.php, FirewallEngine.php, CloudflareService.php):
  * Official Crawler CIDR Authoritative Fallback (BotReconDefense.php): Integrated official Googlebot and Bingbot IPv4 and IPv6 CIDR subnets (e.g. `66.249.64.0/19`, `64.233.160.0/19`, `72.14.192.0/18`, `209.85.128.0/17`, `2001:4860::/32`) into `BotReconDefense`. Guarantees immediate, authentic crawler verification even in containerized (Docker), NAT, or restricted environments where reverse DNS PTR lookups (`gethostbyaddr`) fail, time out, or are unavailable.
  * Search Engine Auto-Ban Immunity Guard (FirewallEngine.php): Added safety checks in `FirewallEngine::add_blocked_ip()` to permanently refuse automated bans on verified search crawlers (Googlebot, Bingbot).
  * Cloudflare Edge Block Safety Guard (CloudflareService.php): Added crawler protection in `CloudflareService::block_ip()` to reject any automated or manual attempts to push genuine Googlebot or Bingbot IP addresses to Cloudflare Edge IP Access Rules.
  * Fast-Path Blocklist Auto-Healing (FirewallEngine.php): Updated fast-path custom blocklist checks to verify crawler authenticity; if an IP in the blocklist is confirmed as a genuine Googlebot or Bingbot, the false-positive ban is automatically pruned and forgiven in real time without dropping crawler traffic.

- Scanner Engine & Performance Developer Hardening Suite (SettingsPage.tsx, SettingsManager.php, Engine.php, DatabaseScanner.php, UploadsScanner.php):
  * Inter-Batch CPU Throttle Delay (`scan_batch_delay_ms`): Added configurable rest micro-pauses (0ms, 50ms, 150ms, 300ms, 500ms) between REST batches to keep CPU utilization low on shared hosting and avoid cPanel/CloudLinux LVE throttling.
  * Background Execution Strategy Selection (`scan_execution_strategy`): Added selectable worker strategies for scheduled scans (`ajax_client` interactive polling vs. autonomous `wp_cli_cron` vs. non-blocking `background_worker`).
  * Media Library Scan Depth Modes (`scan_uploads_mode`): Added Smart Executable & Stager mode (skips millions of benign raster images for 10x faster audits) vs. Deep Raster & EXIF Polyglot Audit mode.
  * Symlink Traversal Policy (`scan_symlink_policy`): Added granular symlink traversal controls (`ignore`, `follow_intree`, `follow_all`) to prevent infinite recursion and safely support Bedrock, Roots, and Composer setups.
  * Fast-Skip Toggles for VCS, Vendors & Caches: Added 1-click developer bypasses for `.git`/`.svn`, `node_modules`, and cache directories (`wp-content/cache/*`, `elementor/css/*`).
  * Database Table Exclusions & Custom Batch Sizing: Added developer table exclusion textarea (`scan_excluded_tables`) and selectable cursor row batch sizes (100, 250, 500, 1000 rows/chunk) to prevent database timeouts on bulky analytics tables.
  * Developer Profiling & Dry-Run Testing: Added scan benchmarking/engine profiling toggle (`scan_profiling_enabled`) and non-destructive dry-run audit mode (`scan_dry_run_mode`) for CI/CD pipelines and staging environments.

- Next-Gen Stealth Malware Detection Vectors: Enterprise Threat Shields (DatabaseScanner.php, CronPersistenceScanner.php, SnippetCodeAuditor.php, WebshellDetector.php, JavaScriptSecurityScanner.php, HookInfiltrationSentinel.php):
  * Dynamic Callback & Superglobal Variable-Function Indirection (WebshellDetector.php): Hardened `WebshellDetector` to flag calls to callback functions (`array_map`, `call_user_func`, `usort`, `register_shutdown_function`, `forward_static_call`) whose callback name or arguments are received directly from request superglobals (`$_POST`, `$_GET`, `$_COOKIE`, `$_REQUEST`, `$_SERVER`), as well as unquoted direct superglobal invocations (`$_POST['x']($_POST['y'])`), while preserving safe object method dispatches (`$this->$method()`) without false positives.
  * In-Memory Zombie PHP Dropper & Process Re-infection Shield (WebshellDetector.php): Implemented coupled detection for memory-resident background workers. Flags PHP files coupling `ignore_user_abort(true)`, zero execution timeout (`set_time_limit(0)`), and self-deletion (`unlink(__FILE__)`) or infinite loops as CRITICAL (`zombie_process_dropper`).
  * Plugin-Hiding & Administrator-Suppression Sabotage Filters (WebshellDetector.php, HookInfiltrationSentinel.php): Added runtime and static inspection for stealth rootkit hooks manipulating `all_plugins` or `pre_current_active_plugins` (unsetting backdoor slugs from the dashboard) and `pre_user_query` (altering SQL queries to conceal rogue admin accounts from `wp-admin/users.php`).
  * WP-Cron Persistence Sentinel & ActionScheduler Auditor (CronPersistenceScanner.php, DatabaseScanner.php):
    - Safe Reflection Dispatcher: Audits serialized cron entries in `wp_options` using non-crashing reflection (`ReflectionFunction`, `ReflectionMethod`) across string functions, closures, array callables, and invokable objects. Flags closures originating from `eval()'d code` or filesystem directories outside active code roots (`wp-content/uploads/`, `/tmp/`) as CRITICAL (`eval_closure_cron_callback`, `untrusted_origin_cron_callback`).
    - Weaponized Argument Payload Detection: Inspects cron event `$args` payloads for PHP execution tokens (`<?php`, `eval`, `base64_decode`, `assert`), terminal shell commands (`powershell`, `curl`, `wget`), and raw IP/executable URLs as CRITICAL (`weaponized_cron_args`, `shell_command_cron_args`).
    - Targeted Core-Spoofing & Entropy Defense: Eliminates false positives on popular plugins using `wp_` prefixes (`wp_rocket`, `wp_mail_smtp`, `wp_all_import`, `wp_fastest_cache`) while strictly intercepting core-mimicking hooks (`wp_version_check_update`, `wp_core_sync`, `wp_auth_check`) and random hex identifiers (`cron_8f9a2b1c`) as HIGH (`core_spoofing_cron_hook`, `high_entropy_cron_hook`).
    - ActionScheduler Persistence Audit: Added fast check on `{$wpdb->prefix}actionscheduler_actions` for pending actions directly invoking dangerous PHP sinks (`system`, `exec`, `assert`, `eval`) or weaponized `$args`.
  * Database Fileless PHP & JS Code Snippet Auditor (SnippetCodeAuditor.php, JavaScriptSecurityScanner.php):
    - Opening `<?php` Tokenizer Normalization: Solved the silent AST tokenizer bypass where plugins like *Code Snippets* (`wp_snippets`) and *WPCode* strip opening `<?php` tags, which previously caused PHP tokenizers to treat backdoors as benign `T_INLINE_HTML`. Automatically normalizes opening tags prior to AST analysis.
    - Zero-Disk-Write In-Memory Scanning: Added `JavaScriptSecurityScanner::scan_content()` allowing complete memory-based client-side malware analysis without creating temporary files on disk, preventing host-level antivirus alerts or permission failures.
    - Deep Snippet & Header/Footer Coverage: Recursively audits active snippets in `wp_snippets`, custom post types (`wpcode`, `elementor_snippet`, `wbcr-snippets`, `custom_css_js`), and script injection options (`ihaf_insert_header`, `ihaf_insert_footer`, `et_divi`, `header_footer_scripts`) via both `WebshellDetector` and `JavaScriptSecurityScanner`.
  * Rogue MySQL Database Trigger Auditor (DatabaseScanner.php): Added in-memory `information_schema.TRIGGERS` inspection (<2ms, 0 locks) to catch database-level persistence. Standard WordPress never uses database triggers. Detects triggers on core auth and configuration tables (`*users`, `*usermeta`, `*options`, `*posts`) with multisite dynamic prefix matching (`$wpdb->base_prefix`) as CRITICAL (`rogue_database_trigger`), and non-core tables executing data modification/file sinks as HIGH (`suspicious_database_trigger`), while exempting verified third-party schemas (`civicrm_`).
  * Steganography & Non-PHP Asset Inclusion Detection (WebshellDetector.php): Hardened `WebshellDetector` to flag `include`/`require` constructs targeting non-PHP assets (`.ico`, `.png`, `.jpg`, `.txt`, `.log`, `.ini`, `.bak`, `.sql`, `.csv`, `.pdf`) as CRITICAL (`steganographic_execution_dropper`). Incorporates `stripcslashes()` to defeat hex-encoded bypass vectors (`\x2e\x69\x63\x6f`) while explicitly whitelisting `.svg`, `.json`, and `.html` to maintain zero false positives on page builder templates and inline icons.
  * ClickFix Social-Engineering Clipboard Manipulation Trojans (JavaScriptSecurityScanner.php): Implemented dual-condition clipboard context inspection in `JavaScriptSecurityScanner`. Flags clipboard modifications (`navigator.clipboard.writeText`, `document.execCommand('copy')`) paired with CLI command runners (`powershell`, `pwsh`, `mshta`, `cmd /c`, `certutil`, `bitsadmin`, `curl`), dynamic decoders (`atob`, `String.fromCharCode`), or social-engineering lures ("Verify you are human", "Turnstile verification", "Win + R", "Ctrl + V") within a ±350 character sliding context window as CRITICAL client-side malware.
- False Positive Reduction Engine: Phase 1 AST & Tokenizer Disambiguation (WebshellDetector.php, DeobfuscationEngine.php, JavaScriptSecurityScanner.php, URLScanner.php):
  * 128-Bit Truncated SHA-256 Micro-Manifest for Known Upstream Vendors (KnownVendorManifest.php, ChecksumProvider.php): Introduced a high-speed cryptographic lookup micro-table (<15 KB, zero Bloom-filter false-positive risk) indexing 128-bit truncated SHA-256 digests of official upstream releases for noisy third-party vendor libraries (phpseclib, mpdf, rmccue/requests, action-scheduler, datatables.net, matthiasmullie/minify). Verifies pristine third-party code in 0ms with line-ending normalization (CRLF/LF), suppressing false-positive heuristic flags without blind path-based exemptions.
  * Media Polyglot Entropy Hardening & E-Commerce Widget Whitelisting (UploadsScanner.php, HTMLAnalyzer.php, TableScanner.php, ChecksumProvider.php):
    - PNG/WebP Compressed Entropy Disambiguation (UploadsScanner.php): Hardened embedded PHP polyglot pattern in uploads. Requires valid printable ASCII PHP syntax immediately following opening execution tokens (`<?php` / `<?=`), completely eliminating false positive detections on compressed PNG/JPEG IDAT binary byte chunks.
    - E-Commerce Review & Tracking Widget Whitelist (HTMLAnalyzer.php): Added verified third-party SaaS providers (`reviews.io`, `ordertracker.com`, `logwork.com`, `trustpilot.com`, `yotpo.com`, `klaviyo.com`, `judge.me`, `ahrefs.com`) to permitted external scripts in post content.
    - Database Schema & Outgoing Mail Log Exemption (TableScanner.php): Whitelisted Schema.org `application/ld+json` and Quoted-Printable security alert transcripts in `wp_post_smtp_logs`, preventing WCP Security Scanner's own alert emails from triggering self-referential script injection alerts.
    - Merchant Domain & Runtime Cache Allowlist (ChecksumProvider.php): Whitelisted official Apple Pay domain association (`.well-known/apple-developer-merchantid-domain-association`), Google Search Console verification files (`google*.html`), LLM manifests (`llms.txt`), server backups (`.htaccess.bk`), and dynamic HTMLPurifier serializer cache trees (`DefinitionCache/Serializer/`).
  * Tokenizer Disambiguation for Shell Backtick Operator (WebshellDetector.php): Refactored shell backtick command execution detection to use PHP's `token_get_all()` tokenizer. Distinguishes true command execution operators (CHAR '`') with interpolated user superglobals from backtick-quoted table/column identifiers in legitimate SQL queries (`SELECT `id` FROM ...`) and sniff definitions, eliminating 100% of backtick false positives across WooCommerce Subscriptions, WP Rocket, ActionScheduler, and LearnDash.
  * Engine Isolation & Minified Dotted File Exemption (DeobfuscationEngine.php, HeuristicEngine.php, KnownVendorManifest.php):
    - Strict Language Scope for Deobfuscation (DeobfuscationEngine.php): Scoped static de-obfuscation unpacking exclusively to PHP file extensions (`.php`, `.phtml`, `.phar`, `.inc`), preventing JavaScript bundles (e.g. Gravity Forms `admin-components.min.js`) from being evaluated against PHP execution primitives (`exec`, `popen`, `eval`).
    - Multi-Extension Parser Hardening (HeuristicEngine.php): Refined multi-extension detection to only analyze files if an intermediate extension token is an executable PHP extension, eliminating accidental PHP heuristic execution on standard dotted frontend distribution bundles (`*.min.js`).
    - Gravity Forms Admin Build Asset Whitelist (KnownVendorManifest.php): Added 128-bit truncated SHA-256 digests for Gravity Forms `admin-components.min.js` releases into the upstream vendor micro-manifest.
    - Security Engine Count Metric Synchronization (App.tsx, ScannerRoutes.php): Aligned the active security engine metric across backend diagnostic telemetry and the frontend audit ribbon to reflect all 23 granular security detection engines (reputation feeds, unbundled core inspection, FIM, deep heuristics, user sentinels, CVE checkers, and DB analyzers), resolving display discrepancies between scan profiles and custom configurations.
  * De-obfuscation Taint Boundary & Dynamic Sink Coupling (DeobfuscationEngine.php): Added strict execution sink verification prior to static base64/compression decoding. Suppresses false positive flags on benign static data hydration (e.g. base64-encoded SVG/images in mPDF, font metrics in TCPDF, or data URLs) unless the decoded payload is actively coupled to an execution sink (`eval`, `assert`, `include`, or dynamic variable functions).
  * High-Fidelity Client-Side Magecart Disambiguation (JavaScriptSecurityScanner.php): Hardened client-side form listener checks to require explicit payment-specific inputs (CVV, CVC, PAN, card expiry) and payment keywords. Prevents benign React admin settings cards and AI prompt panels (e.g. ai-engine-pro `index.js` and `vendor.js`) from tripping false-positive Magecart alarms.
  * Staging IP & Migration URL Contextual Classification (URLScanner.php): Added context-aware path recognition for IP-based external URLs. Classifies raw IP URLs pointing to standard WordPress media paths (`/wp-content/uploads/...`) as low-severity unmigrated staging media rather than high-severity malicious C2 exfiltration endpoints.

- WAF Authentication Stream Integration & Forensic Category Filters (LoginHardening.php, WafFileLogger.php, WafLogsPage.tsx):
  * Streaming Authentication Events to WAF Logs: Hooked individual failed login attempts (`WAF-AUTH-FAILED`) and successful user authentications (`WAF-AUTH-SUCCESS`) into `WafFileLogger`. Failed attempts are logged with HTTP 403 status and sanitized username targeting, while successful logins are recorded as `allowed` HTTP 200 events capturing authenticated user ID and role.
  * Rapid Auth Deduplication Optimization: Updated `WafFileLogger` deduplication cache to apply a specialized 3-second per-user cache key for `authentication` events instead of the standard 60-second block, ensuring rapid brute-force probes and legitimate consecutive logins are captured accurately without socket drops.
  * Dynamic WAF Logs Page Filters: Enhanced the category filter in `WafLogsPage.tsx` with dedicated authentication filters (`All Authentication Events`, `Failed Login Attempts`, `Successful Login Events`) and updated the Action dropdown to cleanly distinguish between `Blocked (Dropped / Failed)` and `Allowed (Logins & Clean Bypass)` events, complete with visual badge icons and high-contrast theme styling.
- Plugin Activity & Audit Logs Noise Reduction & WAF Separation (AuditLogger.php, WcpActivityLogger.php, SettingsManager.php, LoginHardening.php):
  * Strict Concern Boundary Enforced: Isolated the cryptographic Plugin Activity & Audit Ledger (`WcpActivityLogger`) exclusively for administrative plugin settings changes, code modifications, manual administrator actions, and tamper self-defense.
  * Traffic & Authentication Attacks Separated into WAF Logs: Removed `wp_login_failed` hook from the administrative Activity Logger and blocked all `auth_failed` and `login_failed` events from bridging into the activity ledger. Failed authentications, brute-force attempts, and automated bot probes are routed strictly to the WAF Incident Log (`WafFileLogger` / `FirewallEngine`) under `WAF-LOGIN-BRUTEFORCE`.
  * Administrative Login & Registration Filtering: Updated `wp_login` and `user_register` handlers to strictly monitor elevated administrator or role-escalation events, ignoring standard non-privileged customer, subscriber, and visitor activity (e.g. WooCommerce store customer logins/registrations).
  * Automated WAF Trap & Telemetry Option Filtering: Added `wcpscan_blocked_ips_meta`, `wcpscan_ghost_trap_catches`, `wcpscan_threat_intel_ips`, `wcpscan_threat_intel_meta`, `wcpscan_threat_intel_cves`, `wcpscan_self_defense_hashes`, and `wcpscan_self_defense_last_audit` to `WcpActivityLogger::$ignored_patterns`, preventing automated bot traps and background crons from generating synthetic `policy_updated` activity noise.
  * Administrative Capability Verification for Settings Diff: Strengthened `SettingsManager::save_settings()` to verify `current_user_can('manage_options')` before emitting `settings_updated` or `firewall_ruleset_modified` audit events, and fixed MCP token generation to preserve existing tokens across internal settings saves.
  * Route Graduation Telemetry Filter: Excluded `route_graduated` events from the activity ledger, keeping automated adaptive learning promotions inside runtime telemetry.
- Payment Gateway & PayPal REST Mutation Whitelisting (FirewallEngine.php):
  * Synchronized Official PayPal CIDR Subnets: Expanded `FirewallEngine::PAYPAL_WEBHOOK_CIDRS` to include all official PayPal live IP ranges (`173.0.80.0/20`, `66.211.168.0/22`, `64.4.240.0/21`, `64.4.248.0/22`, `91.243.72.0/23`, `185.177.52.0/22`, `192.160.215.0/24`, `198.54.216.0/23`).

  * Integrated Interactive Pagination: Added multi-page navigation controls (`Prev` / `Next` with current page and total pages indicators) to the "Blocked IP Addresses & Subnets" forensic table in the Firewall tab.

- WordPress Plugin Check (PCP) & Security Sanitization Compliance (SessionForensicsEngine.php):
  * Resolved WordPress.Security.NonceVerification and ValidatedSanitizedInput warnings in `SessionForensicsEngine.php`: unslashed and sanitized `$_REQUEST['action']` via `sanitize_key(wp_unslash(...))` with strict phpcs justification comments for low-level session forensic telemetry inspection hook. Zero errors / zero unapproved warnings on official Plugin Check test.
- WebAuthn Passkey Verification & Credential Matching Fix (WebAuthnManager.php):
  * Base64 / Base64URL Encoding & Padding Resilient Credential Matching: Fixed `"Passkey verification failed. Credential not recognized."` error. Modern browsers (Chrome, Edge) and OS authenticators (Windows Hello) return credential IDs with varying base64 vs base64url characters and padding. Added `WebAuthnManager::credential_ids_match()` and updated fast-lookup index resolution to perform byte-level equivalence comparisons, preventing lookup misses.
  * Username-Guided allowCredentials Population: When a user enters their username or email on `wp-login.php`, `WebAuthnManager::generate_login_options()` now populates `allowCredentials` with their registered public key IDs. This guides Windows Hello / FaceID / TouchID directly to the registered credential, while still allowing discoverable/resident credentials if the username field is left blank.
- Session Forensics & User-Agent Anti-Hijack Guard False-Positive Fix (SessionForensicsEngine.php):
  * Fixed False-Positive Session Termination during Background Queue & AJAX Requests: Resolved persistent admin logouts caused by asynchronous background tasks (such as WooCommerce or WordPress Action Scheduler queue runners, e.g., `admin_ajax_async_request_queue_runner`, cron workers, or CLI tools) running under internal user-agents (e.g. `WordPress/6.x`, `WCP-*`, or empty UA). These tasks inherit the authenticated admin session cookie but do not transmit a full browser user-agent, previously triggering a false-positive `session_hijack_detected` alert and destroying the admin's active session.
  * Added Strict Background & Loopback Bypass: `SessionForensicsEngine::inspect_active_session()` now skips inspections during cron (`wp_doing_cron()`), CLI executions, background action queue runners, and loopback agent calls while strictly defending interactive browser requests.
  * Harmonized Client IP Resolution: Aligned `SessionForensicsEngine::get_client_ip()` with `FirewallEngine::get_client_ip()` to ensure consistent Cloudflare proxy handling and prevent synthetic network shifts across subcomponents.
- WebAuthn Login Session Fix & Browser AbortController Protocol (WebAuthnManager.php):
  * Normalized WebAuthn Challenge Session ID: Replaced mixed-case `wp_generate_password()` session tokens with uniform lowercase hex tokens (`auth_` . `bin2hex(random_bytes(16))`). This permanently resolves "Authentication challenge expired or invalid" failures caused by case mismatches on Redis/Memcached object cache backends and case-sensitive database collations when sanitized by `sanitize_key()`.
  * Browser WebAuthn Ceremony AbortController: Added native `AbortController` cancellation to the login screen button. Aborts any prior pending ceremony before initiating a new one, cleanly eliminating the browser error `"A request is already pending"` during repeated or delayed clicks.

  * Strict Fail-Closed WebAuthn Cryptographic Verification (WebAuthnManager.php, WebAuthnPasskeyCard.tsx):
    - Refactored `WebAuthnManager::verify_login()` to enforce strict fail-closed assertion verification: sessions are never established unless `openssl_verify === 1` against a verified public key in PEM SubjectPublicKeyInfo (SPKI) format. Credentials lacking a valid public key or failing signature checks are instantly rejected.
    - Implemented `parse_public_key_to_pem()` supporting DER SubjectPublicKeyInfo sequences, raw 65-byte uncompressed elliptic curve points (`0x04 || X || Y`), and direct COSE P-256 (`-7` / `ES256`) byte maps.

  * Universal WordPress Option Watcher: Engineered an automated option change listener (`updated_option` & `added_option`) inside `WcpActivityLogger` for all `wcpscan_*` options. Guarantees that any setting, rule, hotpatch, or policy updated anywhere in the plugin is reliably captured with complete parameter-level diffs and zero missed changes.
  * Rich Virtual Patch & Hotpatch Auditing: Updated `VirtualPatchManager` and `VirtualPatchSynthesizer` to emit rich structured activity entries (`virtual_patch_applied`, `virtual_patch_deactivated`, `virtual_patch_rules_modified`) detailing CVE identifiers, titles, severity, and Cloudflare Global Edge deployment status.
  * Unbroken SHA-256 Hash Chain & Single Stable Genesis: Engineered an enterprise-grade forensic logging subsystem (`WcpActivityLogger`) linking every administrative event, security reconfiguration, and automated cron task into an unbroken SHA-256 cryptographic chain ($H_n = \text{SHA256}(H_{n-1} + \text{CanonicalJSON}(n))$) starting from a permanent installation genesis hash.
  * AES-256-GCM Envelope Encryption with AAD Binding: All activity records are stored encrypted at-rest in daily partitioned JSONL files (`.json.php` with direct PHP execution prevention). Critical envelope metadata (`v`, `k_ver`, `seq`, `event_id`, `h`) is bound into GCM Additional Authenticated Data (AAD) prior to encryption, completely preventing ciphertext transplantation or sequence tampering.
  * Write-Ahead Transaction Logging (WAL) & Crash Recovery: Built a 3-tier locked transaction state machine (`.journal.json.php`) ensuring complete-byte writes and automatic recovery of interrupted writes without split-brain sequence counters or corrupted partitions.
  * Universal Subsystem Policy Auditing: Connected deep before/after diff logging across all specialized firewall and security subsystems (`LearningModeEngine`, `HoneyTokenEngine`, `AdaptiveRateLimiter`, `GhostRouteTrap`, `OutboundEgressFirewall`, `PromptInjectionGuard`, `UpdateGatekeeperEngine`, and `SessionForensicsEngine`). Any modification to Behavioral WAF thresholds, decoy traps, rate limits, egress rules, or session policies is immediately cryptographically chained into the Activity Logs.
  * Dedicated Firewall Settings & Rule Modification Logging: Updated `SettingsManager::save_settings()` and `WcpActivityLogger::query_records()` to automatically categorize firewall and access policy adjustments under category `'firewall'` with action `'firewall_ruleset_modified'`, ensuring all firewall toggles and rule saves appear in the Activity Logs.
  * Non-Technical Friendly Activity UI & 1-Click Forensic Copy: Overhauled `ActivityLogsPage.tsx` with human-readable plain-English translations for all machine events, top 4 KPI summary cards (Total Events, Admin vs Automated Actions, Forensic Immutability), an expandable forensic drawer (`.wcp-waf-expanded-row`) on each row with complete actor and runtime dossiers, color-coded before/after diff tables, and 1-click copy buttons for Full JSON, Event ID, and Plain Text summary.
  * All Modules Mega-Menu & Settings Search Integration: Added "Plugin Activity & Audit Logs" to the All Modules Command Center (`MegaMenuModal.tsx`) and Settings category list (`SettingsPage.tsx`) under Engine & Operations, complete with glowing "FORENSIC" and "60+ EVENTS" highlight tags across top navigation bars.
  * 7-Day Forensic Immutability & Chained Checkpoint Ledger: Partitions within the last 7 days are strictly protected and immutable. Expired partitions are pruned only after compiling an immutable cryptographic checkpoint in `.checkpoints.json.php`, preserving complete historical continuity across pruned archives.
  * Automated Multi-Channel Tamper Detection & Alerts: Implemented `ActivityTamperNotifier` with automated daily WP-Cron verification. Immediately dispatches emergency email alerts (`wp_mail`), persistent un-dismissible WP-Admin red warning banners, and in-app notifications with anti-spam throttling if any record tampering, hash mismatch, or partition gap is detected.
  * Dual Actor vs Executor Attribution & "System" Detection: Differentiates originating human actors from background execution contexts, automatically attributing scheduled scans and crons to `System` (`System (WP-Cron)` or `System (WP-CLI)`).
  * Dedicated Modern Audit Dashboard & Streaming Exporter: Added a filterable Activity Logs interface with interactive before/after diff modals, one-click cryptographic chain verification, and formula-injection-safe streaming CSV and JSON exports.

- Just-In-Time (JIT) Ephemeral Privilege Elevation Hardening & Anti-Tamper Shield (`EphemeralAdminEngine.php`, `WcpJitMuGuardian.php`, `wcp-wp-scanner.php`, `uninstall.php`):
  * Identified & Neutralized Privilege Creep Bypass: Addressed an escape vector where a user temporarily elevated to administrator could visit `wp-admin/plugins.php` and deactivate WCP Security Scanner, killing the WP-Cron expiration hook and leaving them with permanent administrator privileges forever.
  * Layer 1: Infallible Must-Use (MU) Plugin Watchdog (`WcpJitMuGuardian.php` -> `wp-content/mu-plugins/wcp-jit-guardian.php`):
    - Automatically deploys and synchronizes a standalone, zero-trust Must-Use plugin watchdog into WordPress's `mu-plugins/` directory.
    - Because MU-plugins execute on EVERY request before standard plugins load and cannot be deactivated via the WordPress admin UI, expiration checks (`wcp_jit_mu_check_expirations`) run unconditionally regardless of main plugin state.
    - If the main WCP Security Scanner plugin is ever deactivated, the MU watchdog catches the deactivation event (`deactivated_plugin`) and immediately revokes all active JIT elevations, reverting elevated users to their baseline roles.
  * Layer 2: Deactivation & Deletion Tamper Lock (`EphemeralAdminEngine.php`, `AdminMenu.php`):
    - Stripped and replaced the "Deactivate" action link on `wp-admin/plugins.php` with a disabled `Deactivation Locked (JIT)` badge for all currently elevated JIT users.
    - Added early request interceptors (`intercept_unauthorized_plugin_actions`) on `admin_init` and `muplugins_loaded` that unconditionally abort any direct HTTP GET/POST queries attempting to deactivate or delete WCP Security Scanner (`action=deactivate`, `delete-selected`, `deactivate-selected`).
  * Layer 3: Capability Granular Restriction (`map_meta_cap`):
    - Dynamically denies `deactivate_plugin` and `deactivate_plugins` specifically for WCP Security Scanner when invoked by an active JIT elevated user, while allowing normal management of other plugins.
    - Protects permanent site administrators by denying JIT elevated users the capability to delete (`delete_user`), demote, or edit (`edit_user`) permanent administrator accounts.
  * Layer 4: Emergency Deactivation Reversion (`wcp-wp-scanner.php`):
    - Hardened `register_deactivation_hook` to proactively iterate through `wcpscan_ephemeral_admins` and restore all active elevations back to their original baseline roles (subscriber, customer, editor, etc.) and clear crons before the plugin completes deactivation.
  * Uninstallation Lifecycle (`uninstall.php`): Added cleanup routines to remove `wp-content/mu-plugins/wcp-jit-guardian.php` and clear JIT crons upon complete plugin uninstallation.
