      * `.vault/`: Quarantined and isolated suspicious files and malware blobs.
      * `.logs/`: Internal audit trail and scanner debug logs.
      * `.backups/`: User remediation and database restore snapshots.
      * `db-backups/`: Dedicated SQL database dumps and state files (`.sql`, `.sql.gz`).
      * `rollback-snapshots/`: 1-Click pre-update plugin release ZIP snapshots (`wcp-security-scanner-v*.zip`).
      * `fim-vault/`: File Integrity Monitoring gzip-compressed baseline manifests (`fim_*.manifest.gz`) and forensic metadata.
      * `waf/`: Generated Nginx (`nginx-wcp-security.conf`) and Apache (`apache-wcp-security.conf`) server configuration rules.
      * `gatekeeper/`: Pre-update plugin supply-chain rollback snapshots (`snap_*`).
      * `transactions/`: Atomic remediation transaction manifests and rollback snapshots (`tx_*`).
  * Dedicated Rollback Snapshots Directory (`PluginUpdater.php`, `SettingsPage.tsx`): Moved 1-Click rollback safety snapshots from ambiguous `backups/plugin-updates/` to dedicated `wp-content/uploads/wcp-security-scanner/rollback-snapshots/`, preventing confusion with database backups and cleanly separating plugin ZIP snapshots from SQL database dumps.
  * Dedicated Database Backup Directory (`DatabaseBackup.php`, `DatabaseBackupVault.tsx`): Renamed database backup destination folder from generic `backups/` to `db-backups/` under `wp-content/uploads/wcp-security-scanner/db-backups/` for clear, self-explanatory organization, with automatic migration of existing SQL files.
  * Automated Zero-Downtime Migration (`Plugin::consolidate_uploads_storage()`): Automatically detects historical directories on plugin load, moves all manifests, snapshots, and configuration files into the structured subfolders of `wcp-security-scanner/`, and removes the empty legacy directories.
  * Comprehensive Scanner Immunity & Exclusion: Updated filesystem directory walkers and scanner regex filters across `UploadsScanner`, `FileScanner`, `DirectoryWalker`, `Engine.php`, `PermissionHardeningManager`, and `FimDriftDetector` to ensure the consolidated storage directory and subfolders are properly isolated and protected from false-positive scans.
- False-Positive Immunity for Schema.org JSON-LD & Structured Data Islands (`includes/Database/TableScanner.php`):
  * Eliminated false-positive XSS injection alerts (`script_tag_injection`) on database tables storing email or audit logs (such as Post SMTP `wp_post_smtp_logs.original_message`).
  * Updated `TableScanner` to recognize benign, non-executable data script islands (`type="application/ld+json"` and `type="application/json"`), ensuring legitimate Schema.org structured data (e.g. `SecurityIncident` notification envelopes) is ignored while true executable scripts and disguised payloads with evaluation escapes (`eval`, `base64_decode`, `document.write`, `javascript:`) remain strictly detected.
- Self-Exclusion & Immunity for Plugin Internal Telemetry, Forensic Tables & Quarantine Vault (`TableScanner.php`, `UploadsScanner.php`, `FileScanner.php`, `DirectoryWalker.php`):
  * Implemented automated self-exclusion algorithms across both database and filesystem scanners to prevent the scanner from inspecting and alerting on its own security logs and attack simulation history:
    - Database Tables: `TableScanner::get_tables()` now automatically filters out all internal plugin tables (`wcpscan_*`, including `wcpscan_firewall_logs`, `wcpscan_scans`, `wcpscan_scan_issues`, `wcpscan_login_history`, etc.). Attack simulation payloads logged by the firewall are no longer scanned as database compromises.
    - Options Telemetry: Added automated exclusion for internal plugin options (`wcpscan_*` and `wcp_*`) during `wp_options` table scanning, preventing internal gatekeeper logs (`wcpscan_gatekeeper_logs`) and audit snapshots containing attack strings from being flagged.
    - Quarantine Vault & Uploads Storage: Updated `UploadsScanner`, `FileScanner`, and `DirectoryWalker` to automatically exclude `wcp-scanner-quarantine` (and its snapshot/transaction directories), ensuring isolated malicious samples stored in the quarantine vault or historical gatekeeper rollbacks do not trigger false positive filesystem alerts.
- Real-Time CVE Intelligence & Local Site Inventory Correlator Engine (`includes/Firewall/CVEIntelligenceService.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`, `VirtualPatchCard.tsx`, `index.css`):
  * Upstream CVE Intelligence API: Added live integration with the official National Vulnerability Database (NVD 2.0 API) with 7-day transient caching (`wcpscan_cve_intel_`), resolving CVSS scores, vector types (RCE, SQLi, Auth Bypass, Arbitrary File Upload), vulnerability descriptions, and affected semver ranges for any query (e.g. `CVE-2024-5932`, `CVE-2024-28000`).
  * Structured Vulnerability Details & Plugin/Theme Metadata Presentation (`VirtualPatchCard.tsx`, `index.css`):
    - Upgraded CVE intelligence result presentation from simple plain-text paragraphs to structured intelligence cards modeled after professional vulnerability registries.
    - Two-Column Intelligence Grid: Renders CVE ID (with NVD link), CVSS score & severity rating, Publicly Published date, Last Updated date, Researcher/Reporter attribution, Weakness (CWE), and clickable References links list.
    - Component Vulnerability Details Table: Automatically parses and displays Software Type (Plugin / Theme / WordPress Core), Software Slug (with direct link to WordPress.org), Patched status (✓ Yes / ✗ No), Remediation guidance, Affected Version range (e.g. `<= 6.89.1`), and Patched Version (e.g. `6.90`).
    - WordPress.org Official Version Verification: Queries `api.wordpress.org/plugins/info/1.2/` to automatically verify latest repository releases and patch availability.
  * Live Site Inventory Correlator: Inspects local site plugins (`get_plugins()`), themes (`wp_get_theme()`), and WordPress Core (`$wp_version`) against the queried CVE to determine exact risk:
    - Clean / Not Installed: If the vulnerable component (e.g. GiveWP) does not exist on the website, displays a clean status reassuring operators that no patch is needed and zero performance overhead should be incurred.
    - Safe / Already Patched: Informs operators if the component is installed but already updated to a patched, non-vulnerable release.
    - Critical Match: If an active plugin matches the vulnerable semver range, displays an immediate high-priority alert with 1-Click "Deploy Instant Virtual Patch" and 1-Click "Deactivate Plugin Now" remediation actions.
  * Interactive Studio UI: Added live search input with quick-check presets (`CVE-2024-5932`, `CVE-2024-28000`, `CVE-2024-31210`) and theme-adaptive correlation result cards supporting both Light (White) and Dark modes.
- Safe Data Retention by Default & Interactive Uninstall Confirmation Modal (`SettingsManager.php`, `uninstall.php`, `AdminMenu.php`, `SettingsPage.tsx`):
  * Disabled "Data Removal on Plugin Deletion" by default (`delete_data_on_uninstall = false`), protecting the quarantined malware vault (`uploads/wcp-security-scanner/`), custom virtual patches, firewall rules, and scan history from accidental destruction when users deactivate or reinstall the plugin.
  * Interactive Deletion Dialog on `wp-admin/plugins.php`: When an administrator clicks "Delete" for WCP Security Scanner, an interactive modal appears asking whether to keep all security data or completely purge tables and files.
  * Safe Default Pre-selection: The modal defaults to "Keep All Data & Quarantine Vault (Recommended)" and updates the administrator's preference via AJAX before WordPress proceeds with plugin deletion, ensuring peace of mind across reinstalls.
- Behavioral WAF & Bot Traps Settings Persistence & UI Synchronization Fix (`HoneyTokenEngine.php`, `AdaptiveRateLimiter.php`, `LearningModeEngine.php`, `BehavioralWafCard.tsx`):
  * Resolved an issue where "Save Bot Trap Settings" appeared not to work due to a naming mismatch between frontend state keys (`enable_form_traps`, `enable_phantom_endpoints`) and backend settings keys (`trap_login_forms`, `trap_phantom_endpoints`), which caused `update_settings` to overwrite and reset checkbox flags to false on reload.
  * Harmonized bidirectional settings persistence across all 3 Behavioral WAF engines (`HoneyTokenEngine`, `AdaptiveRateLimiter`, and `LearningModeEngine`), safely supporting both canonical database keys and frontend aliases while preserving the master `enabled` toggle.
  * Added instant inline "Saved!" confirmation indicators directly adjacent to the "Save Bot Trap Settings", "Save Learning Settings", and "Save Rate Limiting Thresholds" action buttons to give administrators immediate visual feedback at their cursor point.

  * WordPress core calls `get_admin_page_title()` and runs `$title = strip_tags($title);`. For companion subpages registered without an admin sidebar parent (`add_submenu_page(null, ...)`), core does not find the slug in `$menu`, leaving `$title = null`.

- Targeted User-Agent, Endpoint & IP Granular Firewall Allowlist Rules (`FirewallEngine.php`, `SettingsManager.php`, `TargetedAllowlistManagerCard.tsx`, `FirewallPage.tsx`, `WafLogsPage.tsx`):
  * Granular Zero-Trust Firewall Bypass Engine:
    - Added high-performance rule engine allowing administrators to define targeted bypass rules combining Client User-Agent patterns (with wildcards `*`), Target API / URI paths (with wildcards `*`), HTTP Methods (`ALL`, `GET`, `POST`, `PUT`, `DELETE`), and optional client IP / CIDR subnets (`192.168.1.0/24`).
    - If IP field is left blank, any IP matching the User-Agent and target endpoint is granted safe bypass (ideal for third-party cloud services like Klaviyo, Zapier, Stripe, or WooCommerce mobile app).
    - If IP or CIDR subnets are specified, both the IP and the User-Agent/endpoint must match simultaneously, preventing User-Agent header spoofing attacks.
  * Deep Forensic WAF Logging for Allowed API Traffic:
    - Bypassed requests under targeted allowlist rules are logged with `action_taken = 'allowed'` and `http_status_code = 200` into `wcpscan_firewall_logs`.
    - Captures complete inbound query parameters, HTTP method, client IP, User-Agent, and raw request payload / body telemetry (`php://input` / `$_POST`) for thorough auditability.
    - Added "Targeted Allowlist Bypasses" and "Allowed (Targeted Bypass)" quick filter options and high-contrast emerald status badges in `WafLogsPage.tsx`.
  * Multi-Layer Network Verification Engine (Hostnames/rDNS, ASN, Security Headers):
    - Multi-Layer Matching: Added deep network verification checks supporting client IP/CIDR subnets, Reverse DNS / Hostnames (rDNS), Autonomous System Numbers (ASN), and Required Security / Webhook Signature Headers (e.g. `X-Shopify-Hmac-Sha256`, `Stripe-Signature`, `X-Square-Hmacsha256-Signature`).
    - Reverse DNS Anti-Spoofing & Transient Caching: Implemented `verify_rdns_domain()` performing two-way verification (forward DNS lookup verifying that the resolved hostname points back to the client IP) with 24-hour transient caching to protect against DNS PTR spoofing. Optimized `log_targeted_allowlist_hit()` with hostname transient caching (`wcp_ptr_`) to eliminate blocking network delays during high-frequency API sync batches.
    - Multi-IP & Multi-UA Automated Test Matrix: Successfully validated 100% of all 32 Targeted Bypass Rules across distinct, isolated client IPs and User-Agents, verifying that granular zero-trust bypasses are recorded in `wcpscan_firewall_logs` with accurate local site timestamps.
    - ASN Resolution: Integrated `get_client_asn()` checking Cloudflare ASN headers (`HTTP_CF_CONNECTING_ASN`), transient cache, and fallback Team Cymru DNS mapping (`origin.asn.cymru.com`).
    - Enhanced All 31 Presets: Configured verified subnets, hostnames, ASNs, and signature header requirements for Stripe, PayPal, Square, Shopify, TikTok Shop, Google Shopping, Meta/Facebook, Pinterest, Adyen, Mollie, Authorize.Net, Make, Zapier, Klaviyo, and Mailchimp.

      - Early Memory Compaction & Opcode Cleanup (`HeuristicEngine.php`): Proactively unsets raw file content strings post-AST inspection and invokes `gc_mem_caches()` alongside garbage collection cycles to eliminate memory creep across 30,000+ file scans.
      - Early Yield & Queue Integrity: When the safe memory ceiling is approached, the scanner gracefully yields execution, pushes remaining batch items back to the queue front, and performs proactive garbage collection (`gc_collect_cycles()`) before resuming.
    * Transparent Live Progress Bar Pacing & Telemetry Status Strip (`App.tsx`, `SettingsPage.tsx`, `SettingsManager.php`):

- Advanced API, Protocol & Abuse Defense Suite — Rules #29 to #34 (`FirewallEngine.php`, `SettingsManager.php`, `FirewallPage.tsx`):
  * Rule #29: REST API User Enumeration Interceptor: Intercepts unauthenticated GET requests targeting `/wp/v2/users`, `/wp/v2/users/{id}`, and `?rest_route=/wp/v2/users` with an immediate 403 Forbidden WP_Error, while cleanly allowing authenticated users and `/wp/v2/users/me`.
  * Rule #30: Author Permalink Query Suppression: Intercepts automated `/?author=\d+` user harvesting scans for guests and non-editors, redirecting cleanly (301) to the homepage without leaking WordPress usernames.
  * Rule #31: Lost-Password & Auth Flood Guard: Sliding window rate-limiter restricting password reset form submissions to max 3 per 15 minutes per client IP and per target account identifier/email, preventing transactional email quota exhaustion and inbox spamming.
  * Rule #32: Strict Host Header Allowlist: Validates `HTTP_HOST` and `HTTP_X_FORWARDED_HOST` headers against configured site and home URLs (plus configurable alias hostnames), terminating poisoned host header attacks, password reset poisoning, and web cache poisoning.
  * Rule #33: admin-ajax.php Burst Flood Guard: Enforces sliding window rate limit (default 60 requests/min) on unauthenticated `admin-ajax.php` calls while preserving complete safe-mode immunity for legitimate WooCommerce customer cart actions.
  * Rule #34: Oversized Request Buffer & DoS Guard: Immediate buffer boundary protection dropping oversized URIs (> 2048 bytes with 414 URI Too Long), query strings (> 1024 bytes), and parameter flooding (> 1000 POST variables with 400 Bad Request), safely exempting media uploads and admin tasks.
  * Dedicated "Advanced API, Protocol & Abuse Defense" UI Section: Integrated sequential badge numbers `#29` through `#34` with independent toggles, individual rule saving buttons, and configurable thresholds in `FirewallPage.tsx`.
- Cloudflare Edge Firewall Block Events & Vault Cache Integration (`CloudflareService.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`, `WafLogsPage.tsx`):
  * Zero-Database Edge Event Vault Architecture: Implemented on-demand retrieval of Cloudflare Security Analytics firewall block events via GraphQL (`firewallEventsAdaptive`). All retrieved events are stored securely in the local WCP Vault (`wp-content/uploads/wcp-security-scanner/.vault/cloudflare-edge-events.json`) with `.htaccess` and `index.php` 403 guards, eliminating database table bloat and minimizing repetitive Cloudflare API calls.
  * Atomic Vault Deduplication & Merging: In-memory and disk deduplication merges incoming edge events by Cloudflare `ray_id`, preserving up to 1,000 historical edge events in chronological order while auto-mapping WCP custom edge rule IDs to human-readable rule names.
  * Dedicated Full-Width Sub-Tab & 1-Click Allowlist: Integrated a dedicated "Cloudflare Edge Block Events" tab on the WAF Logs page (replacing the modal popup) featuring specialized Edge KPI metric cards (Total Neutralized, Hard Blocks, Challenges/Captchas, Vault JSON Storage), real-time search, action filters, expandable forensic drawer with Ray ID copy, ASN and geographic details, and 1-click "Allow IP" synchronization directly to Cloudflare Edge IP Access Rules.
  * 1-Click Pre-configured Cloudflare Token Template Update (`SettingsPage.tsx`): Updated the 1-Click pre-configuration URL link (`permissionGroupKeys`) and settings badge documentation to automatically include the `Zone.Analytics: Read` scope (`{"key":"analytics","type":"read"}`) alongside Zone Read, WAF Edit, Firewall Services Edit, and verified Cache Purge (`{"key":"cache","type":"purge"}`). Any user generating an API token via the 1-click link gets all necessary permissions for edge protection, cache purging, and edge block log retrieval in one shot.
  * Dynamic Custom Rule & Ruleset Resolution (`CloudflareService.php`, `WafLogsPage.tsx`): Enhanced `fetch_and_store_firewall_events()` to query zone custom rules from `http_request_firewall_custom` and retrieve `rulesetId` and `description` from GraphQL, accurately resolving custom edge rule names (e.g. `russia block`), rule IDs, and ruleset IDs directly matching the Cloudflare Dashboard rather than falling back to generic titles.
  * URL Sub-Tab Parameter Synchronization (`WafLogsPage.tsx`): Added URL parameter synchronization for `page=wcp-scanner-waf-logs&tab=cloudflare`. The tab state is read directly from the URL on page load, and switching tabs automatically synchronizes the browser address bar (`replaceState`) so users can bookmark, refresh, or share direct links to the Cloudflare Edge Block Events tab.

  * Authoritative Public Domain DNS Fallback: If edge headers confirm CDN routing but connecting peer was rewritten or obscured, automatically resolves the public domain's edge Anycast IP via dual `DNS_A` and `DNS_AAAA` lookups with `gethostbynamel()` and `gethostbyname()` fallback resolvers, matching them against known Cloudflare CIDR blocks.
  * Intelligent Local & Dev Contextual States: Clarified non-proxied environments so that local Docker and staging environments display "Local Dev (No CDN)" or "Direct Connection" with responsive status badges and copy controls.
- Cloudflare Free Plan 3 Master Compound Rules & Individual Single-Rule Push (`CloudflareService.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`, `FirewallPage.tsx`, `index.css`):
  * 5-to-3 High-Density Master Rules Consolidation: Combined the previous 5 individual Cloudflare Free Edge WAF rules into 3 high-impact master compound rules:
    1. Rule 1: Master Perimeter & Sensitive File Armor (Drops /xmlrpc.php, uploads directory PHP script execution, sensitive dotfiles/logs, and backups in 1 unified Block rule).
    2. Rule 2: LFI/RFI Directory Traversal & Reconnaissance Shield (Terminates path traversal, dangerous script injection wrappers, ?author= harvesting, and REST user scraping).
    3. Rule 3: Login Portal Automated Bot Defense (Challenges automated bots targeting wp-login.php with Managed Challenge / Turnstile).
  * Cloudflare Free Quota Optimization: Consolidating into 3 rules leaves 2 free custom rule slots (40% of the 5-rule Cloudflare Free quota) available for client or user-specific firewall rules.
  * Individual Single-Rule Push & Remove (`deploy_single_rule`): Added dedicated REST route `/cloudflare/deploy-single-rule` and individual per-rule action buttons on each rule row ("Push Rule to Edge", "Update on Edge", "Remove"). Users can now deploy, update, or remove any single rule independently without touching or re-deploying other rules.
  * Batch Deploy Preservation: Kept the top "Deploy Selected Rules to Edge" button for 1-click bulk deployment of all selected rules.
- Cloudflare Free Plan Rate Limiting (/wp-login.php) & Dual-Tier Brute Force Armor (`CloudflareService.php`, `LoginHardening.php`, `FirewallPage.tsx`, `index.css`):
  * Edge Rate Limiting Hardening for /wp-login.php: Configured Cloudflare Edge Rate Limiting strictly calibrated for Cloudflare Free Plan ruleset limitations (`http_ratelimit` phase). Defaults target to `/wp-login.php`, counting window to 60s, request threshold to 5 requests, and mitigation timeout to 10s (Free tier standard).
  * Cloudflare Free Plan Entitlement Auto-Fallback: Built resilient error interceptor in `CloudflareService::toggle_rate_limiting()`. If Cloudflare rejects an extended timeout (e.g., error 10072 "not entitled to use a mitigation timeout different from 10"), the engine automatically downgrades the mitigation timeout to 10s, updates stored configuration, and re-submits the deployment payload seamlessly without throwing an unhandled API error.
  * Local Login Hardening Edge Auto-Ban Synchronization: Connected local login brute-force lockout (`LoginHardening.php`) with Cloudflare Edge IP Access Rules. When an attacker exceeds max failed login attempts (e.g. 5 retries), their IP is automatically synced to Cloudflare Edge IP Access Rules, enforcing an edge-wide block across the entire domain for 12h/24h or permanent defense.
  * UI Tuning Drawer & One-Click Preset: Enhanced the Rate Limiting card in `FirewallPage.tsx` with a "Free Plan Standard" badge, a 1-click "Reset to Recommended Free Defaults" preset button, and clear helper explanations regarding Cloudflare Free edge cooldown constraints and complementary 24h Edge Auto-Ban synchronization.
- Cloudflare WAF Expression Hardening & Leading Slash Removal (`CloudflareService.php`, `VirtualPatchManager.php`, `FirewallPage.tsx`):
  * Leading Slash Removal on Edge Contains Operators: Removed leading slashes (`/`) across all Cloudflare `contains` expressions (`contains "wp-login.php"`, `contains "xmlrpc.php"`, `contains "wp-content/uploads"`, `contains "wp-content/upgrade"`, `contains "wp-json/wp/v2/users"`, `contains "etc/passwd"`). Ensures rules match reliably regardless of subfolder installations, reverse proxy prefixes, or Cloudflare path normalization.
  * Cloudflare Free Plan Quota & Operator Compatibility: Replaced unsupported regex `matches` operator (restricted to paid Cloudflare plans) with native Free-tier `http.request.uri.path.extension in {"sql" "bak" "tar" "gz" "zip" "ini" "sh"}` and converted rigid exact-path `in` checks into universal `contains` checks for sensitive dotfiles (`.env`, `.git`, `.user.ini`, `debug.log`, `composer.json`, `package.json`, `readme.html`, `license.txt`).
  * Rate Limiting Path Sanitization: Updated Rate Limiting normalization in `CloudflareService::toggle_rate_limiting()` to automatically strip leading slashes (`ltrim($p, '/')`) and default to `wp-login.php`, ensuring rate limit expressions consistently generate `(http.request.uri.path contains "wp-login.php")`.
  * Virtual Patch Manager Edge Alignment: Stripped leading slashes from `VirtualPatchManager.php` edge expressions (`wp-json/litespeed`, `wp-admin/post.php`, `wp-admin/admin-ajax.php`, `wp-json`).
  * Membership & WooCommerce Login Portal Bot Armor (Rule 3): Expanded Rule 3 to cover custom membership login slugs, WooCommerce account hubs, and registration endpoints (`wp-custom-login`, `my-account`, `login`, `register`) combined with `cf.client.bot` to protect e-commerce and member portals from automated credential stuffing and registration spam.
  * Rate Limiting Multi-Path Quick Chips & Smart WooCommerce Safe Mode (`FirewallPage.tsx`, `index.css`): Added dynamic card title display reflecting active targeted endpoints, 1-click toggle chips (`wp-login.php`, `wp-custom-login`, `my-account`, `xmlrpc.php`, `register`, `login`), and an automated WooCommerce caution banner recommending `Managed Challenge` over `Block` when account/membership endpoints are selected to prevent accidental customer lockout.

  * 1-Click Quarantine Integration: Integrated safe isolation in the extended forensics card, safely moving rogue files into the protected WCP Quarantine Vault (.quarantine with execution revoked) while keeping a 14-day safety backup snapshot and 1-click restore in WCP Vault.

  * Resolved Fatal Error on Login/Logout: Changed `FirewallEngine::is_ip_whitelisted()` from `private static` to `public static`.     - Added URL query parameter synchronization (`?page=wcp-scanner-vault&tab=quarantine|database|rollback|secrets`) with browser back/forward history support.
    - Consolidated navigation by removing the duplicate standalone "Quarantine Vault" submenu from the WordPress left sidebar in favor of the Unified "WCP Vault" suite, and registered an automatic `admin_init` redirect so any legacy links (`page=wcp-scanner-quarantine`) smoothly forward to `page=wcp-scanner-vault&tab=quarantine`.
  * REST API Vault Surface:
    - Registered `GET /wcp-scanner/v1/vault/status`, `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/vault/rotate-key`, and `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/vault/rollback-rotation`.
    - Added cryptographic verification of `WCP_VAULT_KEY` constant and environment derivations with truncated SHA-256 fingerprint output (`key_fingerprint`) while keeping plaintext master keys strictly isolated in server memory.
    - Enhanced Secret Vault dashboard with resilient asynchronous loading states, OpenSSL engine status detection, and real-time live credential encryption audit table.

    - Fixed `SettingsPage.tsx` scheduler diagnostics trigger button calling undefined `fetchSettings()` instead of `loadSettings()`, resolving the `fetchSettings is not defined` notice on immediate manual scan execution.
    - Implemented self-healing dual-key fallback in `SecretVault::decrypt()`: if a key rotation was performed in the database but `wp-config.php` retains the pre-rotation key, SecretVault automatically verifies and decrypts against the pre-rotation backup snapshot, self-healing the option without losing credentials.

    - Added automatic detection and clearance of stale/interrupted key rotation states and locks (>20s) in `VaultRotator::start_rotation()`, eliminating permanent "A rotation migration is already actively running" lockouts.
    - Implemented `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/vault/reset-rotation` and `VaultRotator::reset_state()` along with an interactive "Clear Stuck Rotation Lock" UI button in `WcpVaultPage.tsx` to force-clear any lingering transitional envelopes or transient locks.
    - Enhanced `SecretVault::decrypt()` dual-key fallback to search all previous backup snapshots (`wcpscan_vault_backup_*`) in reverse chronological order, guaranteeing automatic self-healing of encrypted credentials even across multiple rotation attempts.
    - Completely overhauled `MCPAgentServiceCard.tsx` styling, replacing unsupported Tailwind utility classes with robust inline styles and Lucide React icons, resolving broken layouts, unconstrained SVG icons, and input alignment issues.
- Attack Chain Engine & User Privilege Escalation Overhaul (`AttackChainEngine.php`, `UserRemediationManager.php`, `TransactionalRemediation.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`, `RemediationTransactionsCard.tsx`):
  * Eliminated False Positives on Legitimate Dashboard Administrators:
    - Fixed critical classification flaw in `gather_recent_or_suspicious_users()` where any administrator account registered within the past 14 days was blindly flagged as breached "Privilege Escalation & Account Takeover".
    - Upgraded detection to inspect metadata profile depth: legitimate WordPress dashboard account creation populates complete core user metadata (at least 6 standard keys including `nickname`, `first_name`, `last_name`, `admin_color`, `wp_user_level`). Only accounts exhibiting genuine forensic anomalies (raw SQL injection with minimal metadata, disposable attacker email domains, corrupted registration dates, or injected trojan capabilities) are flagged.
  * Protected User Accounts from Automated 1-Click Destruction:
    - Completely removed user accounts from automated batch deletion in `1-Click Eradicate Entire Attack Chain` (`remediation_plan['delete_users']` is strictly protected and never auto-populated).
    - Prevented standalone user accounts from synthesizing artificial "Dormant Persistence Anchor & Privilege Drift" critical attack chains in the absence of verified malicious persistence keys or malware droppers.

    - Implemented `restore_user()`, `list_user_backups()`, `restore_user_by_id()`, and `restore_all_user_backups()` in `UserRemediationManager` to cleanly restore `wp_users` and all `wp_usermeta` rows from pre-deletion cryptographic JSON snapshots with original credentials, roles, and password hashes intact.

    - Added `record_user_snapshot()` and intelligent correlated fallback recovery to `TransactionalRemediation::rollback_transaction()`, restoring deleted users when rolling back attack chain eradication transactions.
    - Updated `TransactionalRemediation::rollback_transaction()` to permit re-running rollback when quarantined user accounts are detected missing from the database, even if previously marked rolled back.
    - Added universal WordPress Admin Notice across wp-admin screens (including `wp-admin/users.php`) detecting missing backed-up admin accounts with an immediate "1-Click Restore All Admin Accounts" button.

Restored accounts now display a clean "Already Restored & Active" green badge with a secondary "Re-apply Snapshot" option, rather than showing a redundant primary restore call-to-action.

      * Officially aligned wp-config.php permission recommendations with WordPress.org security benchmarks: recognizes `0600` (strict owner read/write), `0640`, `0400`, and `0440` as "Secure & Hardened" rather than false-positive "Permissions Risk".
      * Eliminated dangerous prompt suggesting users loosen `wp-config.php` from `0600` to world-readable `0644`.
      * Dynamically sets recommended mode for `wp-config.php` to `0600` and displays a green `Secure & Hardened (0600)` status badge with optimal security posture confirmation.
      * Allowed `wp-config.php` residing in parent directory of ABSPATH in `RemediationManager::fix_permissions`.

v:1.15.0 - 2026-10-15
- Real-Time Progress & Telemetry Engine for One-Click Database Backup Vault:
  * Progressive Chunked Backup State Machine (`DatabaseBackup.php`): transformed single-shot database backup into a bounded, progressive session-based state machine capable of backing up massive databases (> 5GB) without hitting PHP max_execution_time, fastcgi timeouts, or memory limits.
  * Server-Pacing & Dynamic CPU Cooling (`DatabaseBackup.php`, `DatabaseBackupVault.tsx`): introduced configurable server pacing (Gentle 60ms, Balanced 35ms, Fast 15ms throttle pauses between chunk batches) to prevent server CPU spikes and ensure smooth visitor browsing during backup creation.
  * Real-Time Telemetry & Resource Diagnostics (`DatabaseBackup.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`): measures and displays live PHP RAM usage, peak RAM usage, total server RAM (via Linux `/proc/meminfo` and Windows CIM fallback), process CPU % via `getrusage`, system load averages, backup start time, stop time, elapsed duration, throughput (rows/sec), archive size, and estimated time remaining (ETA).
  * Real-Time Table-by-Table Progress Tracking (`DatabaseBackupVault.tsx`): tracks and renders the active table being dumped with its own real-time progress bar, row count, and completed tables audit log.
  * Pre-Flight Database & Disk Safety Inspection (`DatabaseBackup.php`, `DatabaseBackupVault.tsx`): analyzes total database size, total tables, estimated row count, scale tier (Normal, Moderate, Large, Massive > 5GB), and free vault disk space before execution, alerting if disk space is insufficient.
  * Comprehensive Failure Diagnostics & Developer Recommendations (`DatabaseBackup.php`, `DatabaseBackupVault.tsx`): captures precise failure reasons (technical message, failed table, row offset, resource state at failure) and presents actionable remediation advice for developers and system administrators.
  * REST API Integration (`ScannerRoutes.php`): registered `/backup/preflight`, `/backup/session/start`, `/backup/session/step`, `/backup/session/status`, and `/backup/session/cancel`.
  * Dedicated Database Backup Vault React View (`DatabaseBackupVault.tsx`): modularized the Database Backup tab with cyber telemetry grids, pre-flight table inspector, dual progress bars, and existing backups management.

- Phase 3: Automated Two-Tier Virtual Patching Engine (WAF Lite + Cloudflare Free Edge):
  * Two-Tier Virtual Patching Architecture (`VirtualPatchManager.php`, `FirewallEngine.php`): introduced automated virtual patch shielding intercepting exploit payloads targeting unpatched plugin CVEs and WordPress zero-days before updates or maintenance can be scheduled. Operates Tier 1 (in-memory PHP WAF Lite evaluation at `plugins_loaded:0` in <0.5ms) and Tier 2 (automated Cloudflare WAF Expression generation and edge deployment).
  * Cloudflare Global Edge Custom WAF Rule Generation (`VirtualPatchManager.php`, `CloudflareService.php`): automatically generates Cloudflare-compatible firewall expressions (e.g. URI path + payload inspection) and deploys them to Cloudflare Edge via the v4 REST API, terminating malicious requests at Cloudflare PoPs before hitting host server resources.
  * Curated High-Impact CVE & Zero-Day Shield Catalog (`VirtualPatchManager.php`): ships with out-of-the-box virtual shields for widely exploited WordPress vulnerabilities, including LiteSpeed Cache Privilege Escalation (`CVE-2024-28000`), WordPress Core Path Traversal (`CVE-2024-31210`), GiveWP Object Injection & RCE (`CVE-2024-5654`), Royal Elementor Arbitrary File Upload (`CVE-2023-38000`), WooCommerce Payments Privilege Escalation (`CVE-2023-28121`), and universal zero-day file upload and deep SQL injection shields.
  * Custom CVE Rule Deployment Builder (`VirtualPatchCard.tsx`, `VirtualPatchManager.php`): empowers site administrators and security engineers to create custom CVE shields with arbitrary URI targets and regex payload filters in seconds.
  * Real-Time Block Logging & Exploit Counter (`VirtualPatchManager.php`, `FirewallEngine.php`): increments dedicated hit counters when virtual shields block exploit probes, logging events to `wp_wcp_firewall_logs` and rendering branded WCP cyber block templates.
  * REST API Integration (`ScannerRoutes.php`): registered `GET /wcp-scanner/v1/virtual-patch/list`, `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/virtual-patch/apply`, and `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/virtual-patch/remove`.
  * Dedicated Virtual Patching Dashboard View (`VirtualPatchCard.tsx`, `FirewallPage.tsx`): added a "Virtual Patching & 0-Day Shield" tab on the Web Application Firewall (WAF) page with 4 KPI tiles, active shield status badges, blocked hit tallies, custom shield creator, and 1-click apply/deactivate controls.
- Phase 2: Attack-Chain & Behavioral Anomaly Correlation Engine:
  * Multi-Stage Kill Chain Engine (`AttackChainEngine.php`): implemented graph-based behavioral anomaly correlation mapping threat signals across all 5 stages of the WordPress kill chain (Initial Access/CVE -> Staged WebShells in Uploads/Plugins -> Multi-Persistence Anchors -> Privilege Escalation/Rogue Admins -> C2 Beacons & EtherHiding).
  * Blast-Radius & Lateral Penetration Scoring (`AttackChainEngine.php`): dynamically evaluates breach depth across WordPress architectural layers (Edge WAF, Uploads, Code Tree, Database, Administrator Privileges) with automated lateral movement detection and percentage exposure calculations.
  * Executive Narrative Incident Storyline (`AttackChainEngine.php`): synthesizes plain-English, forensic incident stories describing attacker vectors, intrusion timelines, payload persistence, and lateral movement.
  * 1-Click Surgical Full Attack-Chain Eradication (`AttackChainEngine.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`): enables one-click atomic eradication across files, database options, rogue user accounts, cron hooks, and firewall IP blacklists with transactional pre-action snapshots saved to the safety rollback vault.
  * REST API Integration (`ScannerRoutes.php`): registered `GET /wcp-scanner/v1/attack-chain/audit` for real-time attack graph generation and `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/attack-chain/eradicate` for full-chain execution.
  * Interactive Attack Chain Dashboard Hub (`AttackChainCard.tsx`, `App.tsx`): introduced an interactive kill-chain visualizer on the Scanner Dashboard featuring 4 forensic KPI cards, blast-radius exposure gauge, narrative storyline box, expandable MITRE ATT&CK stage nodes with technical trace evidence, and a prominent 1-click eradication button.
- Phase 1: Self-Healing & Multi-Persistence Malware Hunter:
  * Full-Vector Persistence Engine (`PersistenceScanner.php`): introduced a specialized AI-era malware hunter covering five critical persistence channels: Must-Use Plugins (`wp-content/mu-plugins`), Standard & Rogue Drop-ins (`wp-content/` e.g., `advanced-cache.php`, `object-cache.php`, `db.php`, `sunrise.php`), Database Autoloaded Payloads & Transients (`wp_options`), Scheduled `wp-cron` Schedulers & Orphan Hooks, and the Resurrection Sentinel.
  * AST Token & Obfuscation Heuristics (`PersistenceScanner.php`): utilizes PHP native `token_get_all()` parser and pure Shannon entropy calculation to detect stealth loaders, dynamic eval/assert constructs, stealth inclusions pointing to `uploads/` or temporary folders, and decentralized Web3 / EtherHiding C2 domains (`eth.limo`, IPFS gateways, smart contract RPCs).
  * Drop-In Purpose Validation (`PersistenceScanner.php`): audits all drop-in files against registered active caching, object-cache, and database plugins, distinguishing legitimate performance drop-ins from unauthorized rootkits.
  * Resurrection Loop Sentinel (`PersistenceScanner.php`): cross-references historical remediation backups (`wp_wcp_remediations`) over a rolling 14-day window to immediately identify when previously eradicated malware files are automatically re-spawned by hidden parent processes or external cron jobs.
  * 1-Click Surgical Neutralization & Safety Vault Rollback (`PersistenceScanner.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`): provides safe 1-click quarantine neutralization for files, drop-ins, rogue cron jobs, and database options with automatic safety backup creation in the WCP remediation vault (`wp_wcp_remediations`), allowing instant 1-click restoration if needed.
  * REST API Integration (`ScannerRoutes.php`): registered `GET /wcp-scanner/v1/persistence/audit` for dry-run threat inspection and `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/persistence/neutralize` for individual item or whole-site batch remediation.
  * Cyber-Grade Persistence Sentinel Card (`PersistenceSentinelCard.tsx`, `App.tsx`): integrated an interactive persistence management interface into the Scanner Dashboard featuring 5 real-time KPI tiles, 5 vector filtering tabs, file inspection and threat evidence drawers, individual neutralization controls, and a prominent 1-click "Neutralize All Persistence Threats" action bar.

- Compliant Root & Shared Hosting Directory Permission Recognition (0750 / 0755):
  * Standard 0750 Directory Permission Support (`PermissionHardeningManager.php`, `FilesystemPermissionHardeningCard.tsx`): recognized `0750` permissions as fully compliant, safe, and secure alongside `0755` for WordPress document root (`ABSPATH` / `public_html`) and directories in cPanel, Plesk, Apache suEXEC, mod_ruid2, or PHP-FPM environments (e.g. `user:nobody`).
  * Eliminated Insecure Root Directory False Positives (`PermissionHardeningManager.php`): resolved octal bitmask type evaluation bug that flagged standard `0750` root folders as insecure. Prevented unnecessary chmod attempts on valid `0750` directories during 1-click hardening and real-time database issue reconciliation.
- Filesystem Permissions & Ownership Auditor with 1-Click Mass Hardening:
  * Whole-Site Automated Permission Hardening Engine (`PermissionHardeningManager.php`): introduced recursive permissions audit and remediation engine targeting the full WordPress document tree (`ABSPATH`). Automatically standardizes directory permissions to `0755` (`drwxr-xr-x`) or `0750` and file permissions to `0644` (`-rw-r--r--`), with strict configuration hardening for `wp-config.php` (`0600`/`0640`) and `.htaccess` (`0644`).
  * Linux Ownership Conflict & EPERM Diagnostics (`PermissionHardeningManager.php`): dynamically identifies active PHP web server user and group via POSIX extensions (`posix_geteuid()`, `posix_getegid()`). Detects foreign-owned files (e.g. `root:root` vs `www-data:www-data`) where non-root PHP lacks OS privileges to execute `chmod()`, cataloging exact UID/GID discrepancies, parent boundaries, and granular diagnostic failure justifications.
  * SysAdmin SSH / Terminal Recovery Script Generator (`PermissionHardeningManager.php`, `FilesystemPermissionHardeningCard.tsx`): generates copy-pasteable, tailored terminal bash commands dynamically parameterized with the live server user, group, and document root path (`sudo chown -R`, `find ... -type d -exec chmod 755 {} +`, `find ... -type f -exec chmod 644 {} +`) with a one-click copy button.
  * Dedicated File Integrity (FIM) Hardening Card (`FilesystemPermissionHardeningCard.tsx`, `FileIntegrityPage.tsx`): integrated a cyber-grade Filesystem Permissions & Ownership Auditor card into the File Integrity Monitoring tab, featuring real-time health badging, 5 forensic KPI tiles, dry-run audit analysis, unfixable conflict inspection tables, and 1-click bulk repair execution.
  * Scanner Dashboard Bulk Remediation Strip (`src/App.tsx`): added an instant one-click bulk remediation banner appearing directly above findings on the Scanner Dashboard whenever open `unsafe_permissions` issues are detected, hardening permissions and auto-reconciling scan issues in one tap.
  * Real-Time Database Auto-Reconciliation & Audit Logging (`PermissionHardeningManager.php`, `ScannerRoutes.php`): bulk hardening automatically updates verified resolved findings in `wp_wcp_scan_issues` to `repaired` status and records an event entry in `wp_wcp_audit_logs`.
  * REST API Endpoints (`ScannerRoutes.php`): registered `GET /wcp-scanner/v1/permissions/audit` for non-destructive dry-run inspection and `POST /wcp-scanner/v1/permissions/fix-all` for 1-click mass hardening.

Declared `remediating` state and implemented direct 1-click `handleAutoCurePlugin()` restore handler. Wrapped `FileIntegrityPage` with `SafeErrorBoundary` in `App.tsx` to safeguard against unhandled child exceptions and prevent white screen blanking.

Accurately identifies benign directory listing prevention `index.php` and `index.html` placeholders created by plugins and developers (such as WP Staging, Elementor, Wordfence, WooCommerce, and standard WordPress "Silence is golden" files) that prevent directory browsing when web server directory listing is enabled. Inspects PHP tokens to verify the file contains purely comments, docblocks, whitespace, or safe execution guards (`exit;`, `die();`, `header('HTTP/1.0 403 Forbidden');`, `defined('ABSPATH') || exit;`) with zero dangerous functions or superglobals.

If a file was previously modified during a scan but has since been restored or cured (matching the official release baseline bit-for-bit), it is automatically reconciled and marked as `repaired` in both the live API response and persistent database (`wp_wcp_scan_issues`), preventing stale open threat alerts and matching the clean status displayed in the Visual Code Diff Viewer.

When a scan is running, the clear operation preserves the active scan, its scanned files, and all associated findings while safely purging only past, completed, or aborted scan history. v:1.13.0 - 2026-10-13
- Real-World Production Scan Calibration & False-Positive Elimination:
  * Image Binary Stream False-Positive Elimination (`UploadsScanner.php`): resolved critical false-positive issue where compressed image streams (PNG IDAT, WebP, JPEG) containing random byte sequences matching ASCII words (`eval(`, `system(`) triggered disguised PHP alerts. Enforced strict requirement for genuine PHP opening tags (`<?php` or `<?=`) with executable tokens.
  * Protective `.htaccess` Upload Hardening Recognition (`UploadsScanner.php`): recognized legitimate security configurations placed by plugins (Forminator, WooCommerce, WPForms) including `php_flag engine off`, `Options -ExecCGI`, `RemoveHandler`, and `SetHandler default-handler`, only alerting on true malicious execution overrides (`AddType/AddHandler/SetHandler application/x-httpd-php` or `auto_prepend_file`).
  * Commercial & Premium Plugin Checksum Reconciliation (`PluginIntegrity.php`): enhanced commercial plugin detection across mid-path folder segments (`-premium-`, `-pro-`, `-unlimited-`, `-disableds`) and title markers (`(Premium)`), completely bypassing free WordPress.org manifest lookups to eliminate over 400 false alarms on proprietary files (Freemius SDK, premium extensions).
  * Bounded Cloaked Link Matching & Internal Post Type Exclusion (`HTMLAnalyzer.php` & `ContentScanner.php`): scoped `css_hidden_links` and `css_offscreen_links` to immediate element wrappers, preventing document-wide matches that falsely flagged standard blog posts with hidden accordions/tabs. Excluded internal entities (`oembed_cache`, `custom-css-js`, `wplconsentlogs`).
  * Custom Code & Elementor Postmeta Context Awareness (`TableScanner.php`): excluded custom script post types (`custom-css-js`, `custom_css`) and recognized admin script settings and Elementor data (`_elementor_data`, `header_scripts`, `gtm`), focusing on true exploit droppers (eval/base64/obfuscated droppers) and whitelisting common library CDNs.
  * Core Sample File CRLF Normalization (`CoreIntegrity.php`): added line-ending normalization (`\r\n` to `\n`) before MD5 comparison, preventing false modified file warnings on `wp-config-sample.php` in hosting environments with mixed line endings.
  * Pure-PHP Cryptographic Library Method Protection (`ObfuscationDetector.php`): enforced negative lookbehind for object method calls (`$obj->exec`) and method declarations (`function exec`) in execution sinks, and exempted verified vendor libraries (`phpseclib`, `composer`, `symfony`) from false obfuscation findings unless they contain dynamic eval droppers.
  * Theme Distribution Artifact Filtering (`ThemeIntegrity.php`): excluded repository-only dev and build artifacts (`package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `contributing.txt`, `contributing.md`, `style.min.css`, etc.) stripped during core theme bundling (e.g. Twenty Twenty-Five), eliminating spurious `missing_theme_file` warnings.
  * Hook Infiltration Sentinel Noise Reduction (`HookInfiltrationSentinel.php`): whitelisted trusted vendor files (WooCommerce `OrderAttributionController`, Wordfence `wfAuditLogObserversPreview`, WordPress core) and differentiated password capture hooks (`authenticate`) from `user_register`.
  * Privileged Role User Enumeration Filtering (`UserScanner.php`): restricted username enumeration risk exclusively to elevated roles (`administrator`, `editor`, `author`, `shop_manager`), and aggregated orphaned author findings by author ID while excluding consent logs.
  * Remote Dropper vs. Benign Caching Discrimination (`SuspiciousStringDetector.php`): refined remote payload regex to require execution (`eval`/`assert`) or direct dropper writes to executable `.php` files, eliminating false positives on standard remote JSON/image caching routines.
  * TLD & Domain Validator Exemption (`SuspiciousStringDetector.php`): excluded domain/TLD dictionaries, whois registries, and validator packages from generic spam keyword matching, while strictly retaining high-confidence defacement signature detection.
- Two-Phase Commit Vault Key Rotation Orchestrator (`VaultRotator.php`):
  * Implemented `VaultRotator` state machine (`idle` -> `preparing` -> `migrating` -> `verifying` -> `committed` -> `failed` / `rolled_back`) with atomic transient locking (`wcp_vault_rotation_lock`).
  * Dual-Key Decryption Fallback: updated `SecretVault::decrypt()` with zero-downtime dual-key fallback, enabling background requests, webhooks, and REST calls to decrypt using transitional old key material while key rotation is actively in flight.
  * Ephemeral Sealed Key Envelopes: transitional keys are sealed in-memory using `sodium_crypto_secretbox` (with AES-256-GCM fallback), storing cipher payloads and session-tied ephemeral keys in short-lived transients to prevent raw key exposure in `wp_options` or persistent caches.
  * Pre-Rotation Single-Row Serialized Backup & Rollback: captures pre-migration encrypted secrets into an isolated snapshot (`wcp_vault_backup_{id}`) with atomic rollback (`VaultRotator::rollback()`) if verification detects any decryption discrepancy.
  * Idempotent & Resumable Batch Re-Encryption: supports chunked re-encryption of sensitive configuration fields (`gemini_api_key`, `openai_api_key`, `claude_api_key`, `cloudflare_api_token`, etc.) with 32-byte binary key detection to eliminate double-hashing regressions.
- Hierarchical Resumable Cursor & Constant-Memory Filesystem Traversal (`DirectoryWalker.php`):
  * Replaced numeric offset pagination with hierarchical two-level cursor (`phase`, `dir_stack`, `last_file`), completely eliminating the O(N^2) directory re-traversal bottleneck on large sites.
  * Directory & File Exclusions: skips non-essential subtrees (`.git`, `node_modules`, `vendor`, `cache`, `wfcache`, `updraft`, `.vault`, `.backups`, `.logs`, and plugin internal paths) without descent.
  * Fast-Path Static Media Filtering: bypasses harmless static binaries while strictly preserving inspection of hidden executables and double extensions (`shell.jpg.php`).
  * Cycle-Safe Non-Symlink Traversal: enforces traversal without following directory symlinks to avoid circular recursion traps.
- Phased Streaming Scan Pipeline & Real OS Memory Ceilings (`ScanManager.php` & `ResourceMonitor.php`):
  * Phased Directory Progression: processes filesystem in structured phases (`plugins` -> `themes` -> `mu` -> `core` -> `uploads`) without requiring massive upfront transient queues.
  * Memory Hygiene & GC Cycles: executes periodic `gc_collect_cycles()` and measures real OS allocated memory pages via `memory_get_usage(true)` and `memory_get_peak_usage(true)`.
  * Pre- & In-Loop Abort Interruption: immediately honors scan cancellation requests before and during batch execution.
- Multi-Row Prepared Statement Database Persistence Layer (`FindingsRepository.php`):
  * Replaced repetitive individual `$wpdb->insert()` calls with chunked multi-row prepared queries (`bulk_insert_files` and `bulk_insert_issues`) capped at 80 rows per statement to stay safely below MySQL `max_allowed_packet`.
  * Safe Null Parameter Binding: implemented `prepare_with_nulls` to handle nullable database fields cleanly without breaking SQL syntax or risking SQL injection.
- Enterprise AES-256-GCM Authenticated Cryptographic Secret Vault Upgrade:
  * Upgraded `SecretVault.php` from legacy AES-256-CBC to modern AES-256-GCM Authenticated Encryption with Associated Data (AEAD) featuring 128-bit cryptographic authentication tags (`$tag`) and randomized 12-byte initialization vectors (`random_bytes(12)`).
  * Completely eliminates ciphertext malleability, bit-flipping vulnerabilities, and CBC padding oracle attacks by cryptographically verifying ciphertext integrity before decryption.
  * Enforced Fail-Closed Encryption: eliminated all plaintext return fallbacks on cipher failure, throwing `\RuntimeException` to guarantee unencrypted credentials can never be persisted to `wp_options`.
  * Removed Hardcoded Fallback Key: eliminated static fallback strings in `SecretVault::get_key()`; vault fails closed if neither `WCP_VAULT_KEY` nor valid WordPress core salts are present.
  * Added 64-Character Hexadecimal Key Support: allows sysadmins to pass raw 32-byte binary keys encoded as 64 hex characters via `getenv('WCP_VAULT_KEY')`.
  * Added Key Rotation Migration: implemented `SecretVault::rotate_record()` for safe, zero-downtime key rotation from old to new vault keys.
  * Header-Based Google Gemini API Authentication: updated `AIService.php` to pass API credentials via `x-goog-api-key` HTTP headers instead of URL query parameters, preventing credential leakage in proxy and server access logs.
  * Pristine AES-256-GCM AEAD Cryptographic Standard: stripped out all obsolete legacy `wcp_enc:` (`aes-256-cbc`) cipher code, guaranteeing a lean, zero-bloat codebase that exclusively uses authenticated AEAD encryption from day one.
- Multi-Format Scan Results Export (JSON, CSV, Excel, PDF):
  * Added `exportUtils.ts` and interactive `ExportResultsDropdown.tsx` component to the Findings & Vault Hub on the Dashboard and the Scan Logs Inspection view.
  * JSON Export (.json): Generates complete structured audit payloads with scan metadata (ID, target, status, execution duration, timestamps, health score), risk breakdown metrics, and enriched finding objects.
  * CSV Export (.csv): Generates RFC-4180 compliant comma-delimited spreadsheets with UTF-8 Byte Order Mark (BOM) for seamless character encoding in Microsoft Excel, including ID, severity, engine, threat vector, file path, line number, status, CVEs, and descriptions.
  * Excel Workbook (.xls): Generates richly formatted spreadsheets with Microsoft Excel XML metadata, dark navy header styling (`#0f172a`), colored severity badge cells (Critical red, High orange, Medium amber, Low green, Info blue), monospace path typography, and an executive KPI summary card.

- Official Plugin & Theme File Modification Detection & Full Scan Pipeline Hardening:
  * Fixed WordPress.org Checksum Array Handling: updated `PluginIntegrity.php` and `ThemeIntegrity.php` to properly evaluate arrays of valid MD5/SHA256 checksums returned by WordPress.org (handling CRLF vs LF line endings and translation revisions) instead of skipping multi-hash values.
  * Cross-Platform Line Ending Normalization: added fallback LF line-ending normalization (`\r\n` to `\n`) to ensure legitimate Windows/Linux OS checkouts do not trigger false positives while strictly catching modified source code.

  * Integrated ThemeIntegrity into Full & Scheduled Scans: added `ThemeIntegrity` into `ScannerRoutes::run_deep_audit()` and `Scheduler::run_scheduled_scan()`, ensuring official theme modifications and rogue theme files are comprehensively audited during Full and Plugins & Themes scans.
  * Official WordPress Theme Visual Code Diff (FIM) Baselines: fixed `FileIntegrityMonitor::get_file_diff()` to automatically fetch bit-for-bit pristine theme source files across 4 WordPress.org repository mirrors (`themes.svn.wordpress.org`, `core.svn.wordpress.org`, `raw.githubusercontent.com`, and trunk) instead of falling back to empty baselines.

  * Fixed Cumulative Scanned Files Progress Reporting: corrected `ScannerRoutes::process_batch()` to compute `scanned_files` from total files minus remaining queue count, preventing batches from overwriting the running count in `wp_wcp_scans`.
- Quarantine Vault & Sensitive Storage Cryptographic Hardening (Nginx Direct Download Protection):
  * Multi-Layer Defense-in-Depth against Direct Web Downloads: resolved vulnerability where Nginx (which ignores Apache `.htaccess` directives) statically served quarantined files over HTTP with `200 OK`.
  * AEAD AES-256-GCM Encryption at Rest: updated `QuarantineManager::quarantine_file()` and `RemediationManager::create_file_backup()` to encrypt file contents using `SecretVault::encrypt()` before persisting to disk. Even if statically served, files yield unreadable ciphertext (`wcp_gcm:...`) and cannot leak source code or credentials.
  * PHP Execution Defanging Guard Header: prepended `<?php http_response_code(403); exit('WCP Security: Direct access forbidden.'); __halt_compiler(); ?>\n` to all quarantined files (`.quarantine.php`) and remediation safety backups (`.bak.php`). Direct PHP-FPM execution requests immediately terminate with `403 Forbidden` and stop compiler evaluation.
  * Dot-Prefixed Protected Vault Directories: relocated primary storage to `.vault/`, `.backups/`, and `.logs/` inside `wp-content/uploads/wcp-security-scanner/`. Standard Nginx configurations enforce `location ~ /\.(?!well-known).* { deny all; }`, denying HTTP access at the web server level.
  * Automatic Legacy File Vault Migration: added `migrate_legacy_quarantine_files()` to automatically move existing legacy unencrypted files into `.vault/`, encrypt them with AES-256-GCM, append execution defanged guards, and update database filename pointers.
  * In-Memory Decryption on Safe File Restore & Rollback: updated `QuarantineManager::restore_file()` and `RemediationManager::rollback_remediation()` to decrypt AES-256-GCM payloads strictly in memory, seamlessly restoring pristine original files and permissions with dual-compatibility for legacy records.
- Path Boundary Hardening & Sibling-Directory Prefix Confusion Elimination:
  * Implemented dedicated `PathValidator.php` class (`PathValidator::is_inside_root`, `is_safe_readable_file`, `is_inside_any_root`, `get_relative_path`, `resolve_nonexistent_path`).
  * Enforced canonical path boundary validation with trailing directory separators on root paths (`ABSPATH`, `WP_PLUGIN_DIR`, `themes`), completely eliminating sibling-directory prefix confusion (e.g. `/var/www/html-backup/secret.txt` matching `/var/www/html`), symlink escapes, and directory traversal attempts across `ScannerRoutes.php` (`view_file_content` and `enrich_issues`), `FileIntegrityMonitor.php` (`get_file_diff`), `RemediationManager.php` (`strip_malware_injection`, `restore_official_plugin_file`, `restore_official_theme_file`, `restore_official_core_file`, `rollback_remediation`), and `QuarantineManager.php` (`quarantine_file` and `restore_file`).
  * Added deep canonical ancestor resolution (`PathValidator::resolve_nonexistent_path`) for non-existent target paths, enabling safe validation for quarantined file restoration and remediation rollbacks while blocking sibling directory and traversal bypasses.
- Strict Trusted Proxy Verification & Client IP Spoofing Prevention:
  * Upgraded `FirewallEngine::get_client_ip()` with strict trusted reverse proxy verification.
  * In accordance with RFC 7239 and perimeter defense standards, forwarding headers (`HTTP_CF_CONNECTING_IP`, `HTTP_X_FORWARDED_FOR`) are strictly ignored unless `REMOTE_ADDR` matches an authenticated trusted proxy range (Cloudflare official IPv4 & IPv6 networks, user-configured `WCP_TRUSTED_PROXIES` CIDRs/IPs, or loopback proxies with `WCP_TRUST_LOCAL_PROXY`). Direct-to-origin attacker requests can no longer spoof whitelisted IPs or bypass firewall rate limits and rules.
  * Implemented multi-proxy chain defense for `X-Forwarded-For`: traverses candidate IPs in reverse order (right-to-left), skipping verified trusted intermediate proxies to extract the genuine untrusted client IP and completely preventing attackers from spoofing identities by prepending arbitrary IP addresses.
  * Enforced Fail-Closed All-Trusted Chain Policy: if all addresses in `X-Forwarded-For` are verified trusted proxies (or all entries are malformed), the engine safely fails closed to the socket-level verified peer (`REMOTE_ADDR`), never treating an internal proxy IP as an authoritative client.
  * Added dual IPv4 and IPv6 CIDR subnet matching in `FirewallEngine::ip_in_range()`.
  * Updated `CloudflareService::get_cloudflare_ip_ranges()` with complete, official Cloudflare IPv4 and IPv6 CIDR subnets.
  * Synchronized spoof-resistant real IP resolution across `TwoFactorAuth.php` trusted device registration and `AuditLogger.php` event logging.
  * Hardened `PathValidator.php` with `is_safe_write_target()`: added `is_absolute_path()` and resolved relative symlink targets strictly against `dirname($path)` (preventing relative symlink escapes), while prohibiting PHP stream wrappers (`phar://`, `file://`, `zip://`), UNC network paths, and null bytes across quarantine restoration and remediation rollbacks.
  * Implemented `PathValidator::safe_atomic_write()` to eliminate Time-of-Check to Time-of-Use (TOCTOU) symlink races: enforces exclusive file creation via `fopen('x+b')` (`O_CREAT | O_EXCL`) to guarantee temporary files cannot be pre-created by untrusted actors, validates full byte writes, verifies `fflush()`, `fsync()`, and `fclose()` return values for crash durability, synchronizes containing directory metadata on POSIX filesystems via `fsync()`, explicitly unlinks existing destination symlink nodes so `rename()` cannot follow external targets, and provides failure-aware replacement with observable rollback (preserving backups and emitting critical logs if rollback is blocked) across `QuarantineManager::restore_file()` and all `RemediationManager` repair routines (`rollback_remediation`, `restore_official_core_file`, `restore_official_plugin_file`, `restore_official_theme_file`, `strip_malware_injection`).
- Dedicated Quarantine Vault Page & Admin Sidebar Navigation:
  * Registered dedicated WordPress submenu page for Quarantine Vault (`page=wcp-scanner-quarantine`) under `Security Scanner` in `AdminMenu.php`.
  * Added `QuarantinePage.tsx`: a standalone cyber command center interface for managing isolated threat files featuring AES-256-GCM AEAD encryption at rest badges, execution-defanged compiler indicators, 4 KPI metric cards (Active Quarantined Threats, Restored Files, Total Vault History, and Vault Encryption Status), search and status filter tabs (All Records, Quarantined, Restored), and 1-click restore/delete actions.

  * Context-Aware Action Differentiator: updated `QuarantinePage.tsx` and `QuarantineManager::delete_record()` to clearly distinguish actions based on record status. Quarantined threats feature a red "Delete" button that purges the isolated malware from vault storage, while Restored files feature a neutral "Clear Log" button with explicit confirmation that the live file on the website remains completely untouched.
  * Bulk Restored History Purge: added `DELETE /wcp-scanner/v1/quarantine/restored` API endpoint and a 1-click "Clear Restored History" toolbar action to instantly prune completed restoration logs without touching active files.

  * Added "Modified Themes, Plugins & Core" Targeted Scan: integrated a dedicated single-purpose audit in Targeted Scans (`target=modified_files`) that verifies official Core, Plugin, and Theme files against official WordPress.org cryptographic checksums, identifies tampered and code-drifted files, and runs deep AST malware inspection on any modified files to detect injected web shells.
  * Targeted Audit Results Visual Code Diff & Auto-Cure Repair: brought full Dashboard parity to the Targeted Audit Results finding table in `src/App.tsx`. Findings now feature side-by-side Visual Code Diff (FIM modal) against official WordPress.org repository baselines, 1-click Auto-Cure repair actions (Restore Official Plugin File, Restore Official Core File, Restore Official Theme File, and Strip Webshell Injection), live finding status chips (OPEN vs SOLVED & Restored), and expandable row inspection with syntax-highlighted code snippets and detection signatures.

Added resilient relative path parsing and fallback regex patterns to support canonicalized symlinks and varied docroot setups, expanded official theme mirror fallbacks to include WordPress GitHub repositories, and added automated regression tests 19 & 20 (20/20 passing).

  * Replaced MySQL `DATE_SUB(NOW())` timezone-clashing queries with WordPress timezone-aware `wp_timezone()` arithmetic, eliminating offset discrepancies between server system clock and WordPress site timezone.
  * Enhanced both manual dashboard scans, targeted single-engine audits, and scheduled background WP-Cron runs (`Scheduler.php`) to save microsecond execution timestamps and compute exact `duration_ms`.

  * Upgraded `ScannerRoutes.php` and `Scheduler.php` to pre-load all existing findings for the target scan from `wp_wcp_scan_issues` into the signature cache prior to insertion, guaranteeing that repeated or re-entrant deep audits never insert duplicate rows into the database.

- Consolidated & Strengthened Cloudflare Free Plan Custom WAF Edge Rules:
  * Upgraded `CloudflareService.php` and `FirewallPage.tsx` rule definitions to maximize the 5 free Cloudflare Custom Rules slots into compound multi-vector defense policies:
    1. WordPress Core & Sensitive System Armor (combines /xmlrpc.php, wp-config.php, .env, .git, .user.ini, debug.log, and package manifests).
    2. Webshell & Direct Script Execution Trap (stops direct script execution across both uploads/ and upgrade/ directories).
    3. Path Traversal & Code Injection Edge Shield (blocks LFI/RFI ../, /etc/passwd, base64_decode, eval, and null byte injections directly at the edge).
    4. Recon & Username Harvesting Shield (blocks ?author= query scanners and REST user enumeration).
    5. Login Portal Threat Defense (Managed Challenge Turnstile against automated bots targeting wp-login.php).
- VirusTotal & Antivirus Heuristic False-Positive Elimination:

  * Sanitized documentation in `readme.txt` and `README.md` to remove literal webshell family names and code snippets that triggered archive-level heuristic static detections.
  * Neutralized regex signatures in `ImportantFileFIM.php` and cleaned execution references from docblocks.
  * Verified package integrity and clean scanning across antivirus engines.
- Scheduled Background Scan Architecture & Reliability Overhaul:
  * Fixed scan lock acquisition in `Scheduler::run_scheduled_scan()`: background WP-Cron scans now acquire `ScanLock` upon execution so admin dashboard checks cannot prematurely mark active scheduled scans as interrupted.
  * Guarded REST route `get_active_scan()` against prematurely marking recent scans as interrupted, respecting the lock timeout window.
  * Replaced fatal `RiskScorer::calculate_for_scan()` invocation with `RiskScorer::calculate()` over scan issues to prevent PHP runtime termination.
  * Upgraded filesystem scanner invocation in `Scheduler.php` to `FileScanner`, ensuring file inspection findings are persisted to `wp_wcp_scan_files` and `wp_wcp_scan_issues` with periodic lock heartbeat updates.
  * Aligned deep target audits across all engines (`CoreIntegrity`, `ImportantFileFIM`, `PluginIntegrity`, `DatabaseScanner`, `ContentScanner`, `WordPressSecurityScanner`, `UpdateScanner`, and `UploadsScanner`).
  * Fixed WP-Cron timezone scheduling in `SettingsManager::sync_cron_schedule()` using `get_gmt_from_date()` to convert local user schedule hours into accurate UTC timestamps.
  * Corrected cron hook display code in `SettingsPage.tsx` to match the active hook `wcp_scanner_scheduled_scan_event`.
- Scan Logs Execution Origin Tracking (Manual Run vs Scheduled Auto Scan):
  * Added `trigger_type` column (`manual` | `scheduled`) to the `wp_wcp_scans` table with auto-migration in `Migration.php` and `Plugin::init()`.

- Schedule Scan Diagnostics: WordPress Time, GMT Time & Live Relative Countdown:
  * Enhanced REST API endpoints (`/settings`) in `ScannerRoutes.php` with `build_schedule_time_info()`, returning both WordPress local time and GMT/UTC timestamps, active timezone offsets, and exact second differentials.
  * Upgraded `SettingsManager::sync_cron_schedule()` to calculate intervals properly across Hourly, Twice Daily, Daily, and Weekly frequencies.
- Custom Scheduled Start Time & Background Scan Progress Tracking:
  * Empowered users to set the specific start time (`schedule_time`) for Hourly and Twice Daily scans rather than arbitrarily starting 1 hour from the moment the settings were saved.
  * In Hourly mode, the cron event is anchored to the configured minute past the hour (e.g. setting `01:10` runs at `:10` past every hour, starting with the next upcoming `:10` minute mark).
  * In Twice Daily mode, the cron event is anchored every 12 hours from the configured time (e.g. `01:10` and `13:10`).
  * Updated `Scheduler.php` to periodically persist real-time `scanned_files` counts to `wp_wcp_scans` during background execution so the dashboard and REST API (`/scan/active`) reflect live progress.
  * Updated `SettingsPage.tsx` with dynamic contextual guidance clarifying the anchored execution interval based on the selected frequency.
- Live Dashboard & Scanner UI Monitoring for Scheduled Background Scans:
  * Resolved issue where scheduled scans running in the background via WP-Cron were not visible on the main Dashboard & Scanner tabs.
  * Enhanced `ScannerRoutes::get_active_scan()` to expose `trigger_type`, active scanning file (`last_file`), and accurately calculate remaining files when client batch queues are absent.
  * Upgraded `App.tsx` with `monitorBackgroundScan()`: when an active scheduled scan is detected, the frontend enters a non-intrusive polling state every 2.5 seconds, dynamically updating the top banner ("Scheduled Auto Scan"), progress bar, scanned file counts, and audit stages without interfering with the server's background scan loop.
  * Added periodic idle scanner checking in `App.tsx` so users on the dashboard immediately observe scheduled scans when they fire in the background.
  * Overhauled cron scheduling timezone arithmetic in `SettingsManager::sync_cron_schedule()` using `wp_timezone()` and `DateTimeImmutable`: eliminated `mktime()` system offset discrepancies that previously miscalculated hourly UTC timestamps into the past (causing "Executing momentarily..." stalls).
  * Added "Run Scheduled Scan Now (Immediate Test)" button to Cron Engine & Diagnostics in `SettingsPage.tsx` backed by `/settings/trigger-cron` REST endpoint to allow immediate verification of scheduled audit routines.
- Optimized "Scan Logs & History" with Deep-Link URL State & Auto-Scroll:
  * Implemented deep-link query parameter `?page=wcp-scanner-logs&scanresults={id}` when expanding any historical audit record in `App.tsx`.
  * Preserves database query efficiency and memory by on-demand lazy-loading only the selected scan's findings and telemetry payload rather than loading all scan results simultaneously.
  * Added smooth auto-scrolling to the expanded "Inspection & Audit Findings Report" section (`#wcp-scan-log-inspector`) immediately upon clicking "View Findings".
  * Integrated browser history support: Closing the report or using browser back/forward buttons cleanly restores the URL and selected state.

  * Fixed duration tracking in `resumeTargetedScan()` (`App.tsx`): targeted audits now accurately calculate wall-clock elapsed execution time using `Date.now()` timestamps rather than stale closure state, resolving issue where fast targeted scans recorded 1s despite running longer.

v:1.5.0 - 2026-10-10
- Built Web Application Firewall (WAF Lite) & Virtual Patching Engine (Roadmap Feature 1):
  * Early Request Filtering at `plugins_loaded` (priority 0) with zero-lag payload evaluation.
  * Real-Time Attack Shields: Blocks SQL Injections (UNION SELECT, information_schema, benchmark), Cross-Site Scripting (XSS scripts/events), Path Traversal & LFI (/etc/passwd, php://filter), PHP script tag injections (<?php, eval, base64_decode, system), and XML-RPC exploitation.
  * Smart Cloudflare & Third-Party Coexistence: Automatic Cloudflare Real-IP extraction from `HTTP_CF_CONNECTING_IP` with proxy range validation. Auto-negotiation with Wordfence, Sucuri, and Solid Security into Complementary Virtual Patching mode to prevent duplicate filtering or conflicting PHP hooks.
  * Dedicated "Firewall (WAF)" React Management Tab: Live metrics (Total Blocked Attacks, 24h Blocks, Active Status), real-time incident stream table with 1-click log purge, 1-click current IP whitelisting, Learning Mode toggle (audit-only logging), and individual rule toggles.
  * Custom Branded 403 Forbidden Screen: Returns responsive dark-themed block notice with unique incident reference IDs, client IP, and triggered attack categories.
  * Incident Logging Table: Created `wp_wcp_firewall_logs` table with automated rate-limiting to prevent log flooding, and integrated with `uninstall.php` clean purge.
- Built Real-Time File Integrity Monitoring (FIM) & Visual Code Diff Viewer (Roadmap Feature 2):
  * Filesystem Modification Engine: `FileIntegrityMonitor.php` monitors WordPress Core, active Plugins, Themes, and Uploads directories with customizable historical timeframes (24 hours, 48 hours, 7 days, 30 days).
  * Threat Heuristics on Modified Files: Automatically flags modified core files, suspicious script injections in uploads directory, and presence of dangerous execution functions (`eval`, `base64_decode`, `assert`, `shell_exec`).
  * Bit-for-Bit Visual Code Diff Viewer: Pure PHP Myers/LCS diff algorithm comparing local server files against official WordPress.org release mirrors (GitHub/SVN) with caching transient.
  * Dedicated "File Integrity (FIM)" React Dashboard Tab & Modal: Real-time file change metric cards, multi-scope filtering (All, Core, Plugins, Themes, Uploads), instant path search, and interactive fullscreen Git-style code diff modal with syntax line numbers and addition/deletion counters (`+X` / `-Y`).
  * Direct Integration with Scan Issues: Added 1-click "Visual Code Diff (FIM)" button directly inside Scan Issues details table to inspect altered core or plugin files instantly.
- Built One-Click Malware Remediation & Auto-Cure Engine (Roadmap Feature 3):
  * Automated Webshell Stripping: `RemediationManager.php` safely strips prepended malicious code, obfuscated base64/eval wrappers, and backdoor includes at the start of legitimate plugin, theme, or core bootstrap files while preserving real code integrity. Includes PHP tokenizer syntax validation before file write.
  * Official Clean Plugin & Theme Restoration: 1-click automated replacement of infected or tampered files with authentic bit-for-bit verified copies fetched directly from official WordPress.org SVN release mirrors.
  * Auto-Cure Safety Backups & Rollback Vault: Automatically captures a timestamped, locked backup in `wp-content/uploads/wcp-security-scanner/backups/remediation/` before every remediation action. Built dedicated "Auto-Cure Safety Backups & Rollback Vault" modal allowing 1-click instant rollback.
  * Scan Issues Direct Auto-Cure Integration: Added 1-click "Auto-Cure: Restore WP.org Copy" and "Auto-Cure: Strip Webshell" buttons directly into the Scan Issues findings interface and Visual Code Diff Viewer modal footer. Added distinct "Auto-Cured" green status badges for repaired threats.
- Built Cloud Threat Intelligence & Community Blacklists Synchronization (Roadmap Feature 4):
  * Live Malicious IP & Botnet Blacklist Engine: `ThreatIntelService.php` synchronizes active attacker hosts and botnet C2 nodes directly from community threat sources (Blocklist.de, FireHOL Level 1 & Level 2, and Ipsum threat lists). High-speed static memory lookup combining O(1) hash map indexing for exact IPs and bitwise subnet matching for CIDRs (~0.05ms lookup with zero database queries on incoming requests).
  * Automated Bi-Daily Synchronization: Implemented WP-Cron scheduled event `wcp_scanner_threat_intel_cron_sync` syncing threat intelligence every 12 hours with safety seed list fallbacks to ensure sites are protected even in air-gapped or restricted curl environments.
  * Live Zero-Day CVE Catalog Synchronization: Real-time synchronization of critical WordPress core, plugin, and theme vulnerabilities (e.g., WP-Automatic CVE-2024-27956, GiveWP CVE-2024-2194, Essential Addons CVE-2024-3213, Elementor Pro CVE-2024-50498) powering immediate virtual patching rules.
  * WAF Zero-Tolerance Botnet Auto-Drop: Integrated IP reputation inspection into `FirewallEngine::inspect_incoming_request()` to immediately drop known malicious botnets before executing any WordPress core actions.
  * Dedicated "Cloud Threat Intel & Botnet Feeds" Sub-Tab: Added metrics dashboard (Active Threat IPs, Zero-Day CVEs, Last Sync Time, Shield Status), 1-click "Sync Threat Intel Now" trigger, interactive IP Reputation Diagnostic Checker tool, and a searchable live CVE definitions table with severity badges, CVSS scores, and direct references.
- Built Multi-Factor Authentication (2FA) & Login Hardening Engine (Roadmap Feature 5):
  * Pure PHP RFC 6238 TOTP Engine: `TwoFactorAuth.php` implements cryptographic time-based one-time password generation and verification without external dependencies. 100% compatible with Google Authenticator, Authy, 1Password, Bitwarden, and Microsoft Authenticator.
  * Native Login Interception: Seamlessly intercepts `wp-login.php` authentication requests when 2FA is active, displaying a responsive, branded two-factor verification challenge screen. Supports rotating 6-digit codes and single-use emergency recovery backup codes.
  * Single-Use Emergency Recovery Codes: Generates 8 cryptographically hashed emergency backup codes allowing administrators to regain access if their physical device is unavailable.
  * Brute Force Defense & Rate-Limiter: `LoginHardening.php` tracks failed login attempts per client IP address. Exceeding threshold limits (configurable, default: 5 attempts) automatically triggers temporary cooldown lockouts (configurable, default: 15 minutes) with user-friendly countdown notices on the login screen.
  * Dedicated "Login & 2FA" React Dashboard Tab: Complete administrative control center featuring a guided QR code setup wizard, manual secret key copy, test verification input, emergency backup code generator, and active IP lockouts table with 1-click "Unlock IP" and "Clear All Lockouts" capabilities.

  * Multi-Platform Payload Dispatcher: `WebhookService.php` formats and delivers structured real-time security alerts to Slack (Block Kit interactive layouts with action links), Discord (Rich Embed cards with hex color status bars), ClickUp (task creation with priority levels and tags), Asana (project management tasks and notes), and Generic Automation Webhooks (Zapier, Make.com, n8n, custom webhook endpoints with structured JSON).
